Method: 将DQN更新使用的小批量数据建模为τ-混合过程，证明该假设在底层轨迹和采样机制满足一定条件时成立；将每次更新建模为带τ-混合观测的非参数回归问题，推导有限样本风险界；从风险界导出样本复杂度。

Result: 时间依赖会导致统计速率下降，在速率指数中引入额外的维度惩罚，反映τ-混合数据有效样本量的减少；推导了τ-混合下DQN的样本复杂度；实验证明独立性假设被系统性违背，回放采样产生近似指数衰减的相关性。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解深度Q学习在依赖数据下的行为提供了理论基础，揭示了时间依赖对学习性能的负面影响，并解释了回放采样的实际效果。

Abstract: Finite-sample analyses of deep Q-learning typically treat replayed data as independent, even though it is sampled from temporally dependent state-action trajectories. We study the Deep Q-networks (DQN) algorithm under explicit dependence by modelling the minibatches used for updating the network as $τ$-mixing. We show that this assumption holds under certain dependence conditions on the underlying trajectories and the mechanism used to sample minibatches. Building on this observation, we extend statistical analyses of DQN with fully connected ReLU architectures to dependent data. We formulate each update as a nonparametric regression problem with $τ$-mixing observations and derive finite-sample risk bounds under this dependence structure. Our results show that temporal dependence leads to a degradation in the statistical rate by inducing an additional dimensionality penalty in the rate exponent, reflecting the reduced effective sample size of $τ$-mixing data. Moreover, we derive the sample complexity of DQN under $tau$-mixing from these risk bounds. Finally, we empirically demonstrate on standard Gymnasium environments that the independence assumption is systematically violated and that replay sampling yields approximately exponentially decaying correlations, supporting our theoretical framework.

</details>


### [253] [Risk-Controlled Post-Processing of Decision Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06479)
*Sunay Joshi,Tao Wang,Hamed Hassani,Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种风险控制的后处理方法：给定一个确定性基线策略，通过选择与基线最大程度一致但满足用户指定损失的机会约束的新策略。最优策略具有阈值结构——仅在切换到回退策略能大幅降低条件违规风险时才偏离基线。利用校准数据选择阈值，在i.i.d.条件下期望超额风险为O(log n/n)，若存在精确安全的回退策略则可实现精确期望风险控制。在COVID-19影像诊断、LLM路由和合成多分类任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测模型通常通过已有的决策策略部署，利益相关者不愿改变这些策略，除非风险约束强制要求干预。因此需要一种能在不显著改变现有策略行为的前提下满足风险约束的后处理方法。

Method: 1) 理论证明最优策略具有阈值结构：在大多数情况下遵循基线策略，仅在条件违规风险大幅降低时切换到回退策略。2) 开发基于校准数据选择阈值的后处理算法。3) 利用算法稳定性与随机过程工具给出理论保证。

Result: 在i.i.d.条件下，后处理策略的期望超额风险为O(log n/n)。当存在精确安全的回退策略时，算法在可交换性条件下实现精确期望风险控制，并提供高概率的近似最优性保证。实验表明该方法能在满足风险预算的同时，比随机混合策略保留更多与基线策略的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的风险控制后处理方法能有效在满足风险约束的同时最大化与现有决策策略的一致性，具有理论保证且在实践中表现良好。

Abstract: Predictive models are often deployed through existing decision policies that stakeholders are reluctant to change unless a risk constraint requires intervention. We study risk-controlled post-processing: given a deterministic baseline policy, choose a new policy that maximizes agreement with the baseline subject to a chance constraint on a user-specified loss. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy has a threshold structure: it follows the baseline except on contexts where switching to the oracle fallback policy yields a large reduction in conditional violation risk. At the finite-sample level, given a fitted fallback policy and score, we develop a post-processing algorithm that uses calibration data to select a threshold. Leveraging tools from algorithmic stability and stochastic processes, we show that under regularity conditions, in the i.i.d. setting, the expected excess risk of the post-processed policy is $O(\log n/n)$. In the special case when an exact-safe fallback policy is available, the algorithm achieves precise expected risk control under exchangeability. In this setting, we also give high-probability near-optimality guarantees on the post-processed policy. Experiments on a COVID-19 radiograph diagnosis task, an LLM routing problem, and a synthetic multiclass decision task show that targeted post-processing can meet or nearly meet risk budgets while preserving substantially more agreement with the baseline than score-blind random mixing.

</details>


### [254] [DARTS: Targeting Prognostic Covariates in Budget-Constrained Sequential Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06608)
*Kateryna Husar,Alexander Volfovsky*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DARTS框架，将协变量获取视为嵌入因果推断的序列优化问题，通过汤普森采样自适应选择最具预测性的协变量，在预算约束下提升实验效率，并理论证明了自适应选择不影响批次级随机化有效性，实证表明能缩小与理想设计的效率差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机对照试验假设预后协变量已知且免费获取，但实际中获取高维预处理数据成本高昂，需要在协变量自适应精度与测量预算之间进行权衡。

Method: 提出DARTS框架：将协变量获取建模为序列优化问题嵌入因果推断设计；使用预算约束的组合汤普森采样器在连续批次间学习最具预后价值的协变量；基于所选协变量进行重随机化和回归调整以降低批次级平均处理效应方差。

Result: 理论上证明了自适应协变量选择不影响批次级随机化有效性，累积逆方差加权估计量达到名义渐近覆盖；获取层的贝叶斯风险界匹配到对数因子下的极小极大下界；实证中系统性地将预算集中在信息量大的特征上，显著缩小与理想设计的效率差距。

Conclusion: DARTS在预算约束下通过自适应协变量选择有效提升实验效率，同时严格保持统计推断的有效性。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials typically assume that prognostic covariates are known and available at no cost. In practice, obtaining high-dimensional pretreatment data is costly, forcing a trade-off between covariate-adaptive precision and a measurement budget. We introduce Dynamic Adaptive Rerandomization via Thompson Sampling (DARTS), which treats covariate acquisition as a sequential optimization problem embedded within a design-based causal inference task. A budgeted combinatorial Thompson sampler learns which covariates are most prognostic across successive batches; selected covariates then drive rerandomization and regression adjustment to reduce batch-level average treatment effect variance. Our primary theoretical contribution is a decoupling result: adaptive covariate selection based on past batches preserves batch-level randomization validity, and the cumulative inverse-variance weighted estimator achieves at least nominal asymptotic coverage. We further derive a Bayes risk bound for the acquisition layer that matches the minimax lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Empirically, DARTS systematically concentrates the budget on informative features, significantly closing the efficiency gap to oracle designs while maintaining strict inferential validity.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Optimal Confidence Band for Kernel Gradient Flow Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05768)
*Yuqian Cheng,Zhuo Chen,Qian Lin*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了核梯度流在最大范数下的泛化误差和均匀推断，在容量-源条件框架下建立了连续和离散核梯度流的收敛速度，并证明其达到极小极大最优速率，进而构建了最优宽度的同时置信带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核回归方法在L2范数下的误差分析已较为成熟，但针对最大范数（supremum-norm）的泛化误差和均匀推断研究尚不充分，而这对构造同时置信带等统计推断任务至关重要。

Method: 在容量-源条件框架下，利用核函数的嵌入指数α₀，对满足源条件s>α₀的连续和离散核梯度流建立最大范数泛化误差的收敛速度，并基于此构造同时置信带。

Result: 建立了连续和离散核梯度流在最大范数下的收敛速度，证明其达到极小极大最优速率；构造的同时置信带宽度收缩速率大于（可任意接近）极小极大最优速率，即具有最优性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了核梯度流在最大范数误差分析方面的理论空白，提供了同时置信带的最优构造方法，为核回归方法的统计推断奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the supremum-norm generalization error and the uniform inference for a specific class of kernel regression methods, namely the kernel gradient flows. Under the widely adopted capacity-source condition framework in the kernel regression literature, we first establish convergence rates for the supremum norm generalization error of both continuous and discrete kernel gradient flows under the source condition $s>α_0$, where $α_0\in(0,1)$ denotes the embedding index of the kernel function. Moreover, we show that these rates match the minimax optimal rates. Building on this result, we then construct simultaneous confidence bands for both continuous and discrete kernel gradient flows. Notably, the widths of the proposed confidence bands are also optimal, in the sense that their shrinkage rates are greater than, while can be arbitrarily close to, the minimax optimal rates.

</details>


### [256] [Time-Inhomogeneous Preconditioned Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06091)
*Alexander Falk,Laurenz Nagler,Andreas Habring,Thomas Pock*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出TIPreL，一种时间和位置依赖的预条件Langevin动力学方法，同时解决多模态分布中的全局模式覆盖和局部模式探索两大挑战，并在理论和实验上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Langevin采样方法在预条件设计上存在全局模式覆盖与局部模式探索之间的固有权衡，尚无方法能同时解决这两个挑战。

Method: 提出时间-位置依赖的预条件器TIPreL，将预条件设计为依赖于当前时间步和样本位置的函数，从而在一个统一框架内同时处理全局和局部采样挑战。

Result: 在Wasserstein-2距离下证明了连续时间和驯化Euler离散化的收敛性，改进了现有理论——在时间和空间依赖的扩散系数且仅局部Lipschitz漂移条件下证明了收敛。在二维病态问题和贝叶斯逻辑回归任务上验证了方法的效率。

Conclusion: TIPreL有效突破了现有预条件方法的局限，实现了全局模式覆盖与局部模式探索的兼顾，并在理论和实验上均表现出优势。

Abstract: Langevin sampling from distributions of the form $p(x) \propto \exp(-Ψ(x))$ faces two major challenges: (global) mode coverage and (local) mode exploration. The first challenge is particularly relevant for multi-modal distributions with disjoint modes, whereas the second arises when the potential $Ψ$ exhibits diverse and ill-conditioned local mode geometry. To address these challenges, a common approach is to precondition Langevin dynamics with problem-specific information, such as the sample covariance or the local curvature of $Ψ$. However, existing preconditioner choices inherently involve a trade-off between global mode coverage and local mode exploration, and no prior method resolves both simultaneously. To overcome this limitation, we propose the TIPreL, which introduces a time- and position-dependent preconditioner. This design effectively addresses both challenges mentioned above within a single framework. We establish convergence of the resulting dynamics in the Wasserstein-2 distance both in continuous time and for a tamed Euler discretization. In particular, our analysis extends the existing state of the art by proving convergence under time- and space-dependent diffusion coefficients, and only locally Lipschitz drifts, which has not been covered by prior work. Finally, we experimentally compare TIPreL with competing preconditioning schemes on a two-dimensional, severely ill-posed example and on a Bayesian logistic regression task in higher dimensions, confirming the efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [257] [Minimax estimation of Functional Principal Components from noisy discretized functional data: the case of smooth processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06417)
*Nassim Bourarach,Franck Picard,Vincent Rivoirard,Angelina Roche*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数主成分分析中协方差特征函数和特征值的极小极大估计。针对具有任意 Hölder 光滑性的协方差核，推导了特征函数估计的非渐近极小极大下界（涉及采样变异、离散化和谱效应），并提出了基于 Coiflet 小波投影的可计算估计器，其速率匹配了样本量和网格分辨率的最优依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对函数主成分分析中的协方差特征函数和特征值估计的理论理解尚不完善，特别是核光滑性与谱结构之间的相互作用未被充分刻画。本文旨在厘清光滑性本身不足以保证极小极大一致估计的问题，并建立联合控制光滑性与局部谱分离的过程类，从而导出精确的极小极大速率。

Method: 1) 定义联合控制协方差核 Hölder 光滑性和局部相对逆特征间隔量的过程类；2) 推导非渐近极小极大下界，将采样误差、离散化误差和谱效应解耦；3) 构造基于 Coiflet 缩放函数和小波积分的可计算投影估计器；4) 通过 Karhunen–Loève 展开建立谱衰减、特征函数正则性和协方差核光滑性之间的联系。

Result: 特征函数估计的极小极大下界呈现 δ_ℓ n^{-1} + p^{-2α} 的速率，其中 δ_ℓ 度量谱困难程度。所提出的 Coiflet 小波投影估计器在特征函数估计上匹配了样本量和网格分辨率的最优依赖关系（对于任意 α>0）。在特征值估计的相对平方误差损失下也得到了非渐近下界。

Conclusion: 核光滑性单独不足以实现极小极大一致的特征函数估计，必须联合考虑局部谱分离。所提出的框架涵盖了若干经典高斯过程和 Karhunen–Loève 构造，能够预测相变和不利的离散化效应。

Abstract: We study the minimax estimation of covariance eigenfunctions and eigenvalues in functional principal component analysis when $n$ trajectories are observed at $p$ common grid points with additive noise. We consider covariance kernels with arbitrary Hölder smoothness and no prescribed parametric decay of the eigenvalues. In this setting, kernel smoothness and local spectral separation play distinct roles: a minimax inconsistency result over the smoothness-only class shows that kernel regularity alone is not sufficient for minimax-consistent eigenfunction estimation. To capture this interplay, we introduce a class of processes that jointly controls the Hölder smoothness of the covariance kernel and a local relative inverse eigengap quantity at the target index $\ell$. Over this class, we derive non-asymptotic minimax lower bounds for eigenfunction estimation that disentangle sampling variability, discretization and spectral effects, revealing rates of order $δ_\ell n^{-1}+p^{-2α}$, where $δ_\ell$ quantifies the spectral difficulty. We also obtain non-asymptotic lower bounds for eigenvalue estimation under a relative squared-error loss. We then construct a computable wavelet projection estimator based on Coiflet scaling functions and a quadrature scheme designed to accommodate arbitrary Hölder smoothness. For eigenfunction estimation, this estimator matches the minimax dependence on the sample size and grid resolution, up to the natural spectral factor, for any Hölder index $α>0$. Finally, we show that the proposed framework covers several classical Gaussian processes and Karhunen--Loève constructions. In particular, a Karhunen--Loève based criterion links spectral decay, eigenfunction regularity and covariance-kernel smoothness, and yields controlled simulation settings illustrating the predicted phase transitions and least-favourable discretization effects.

</details>


### [258] [Time-sensitive anytime-valid testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06521)
*Eugenio Clerico,Tobias Wegel,Iskander Azangulov,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时间敏感的测试-投注框架，通过赋予拒绝时间奖励并最大化其期望值来优先考虑早期拒绝，适用于硬截止和软时间偏好，并推导出最优控制问题的Bellman表示，以及针对硬截止和指数衰减奖励的优化准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意有效检验中，拒绝假设只有在足够早发生时才有实际意义，但现有框架未充分考虑时间敏感性。作者旨在设计一种能奖励早期拒绝并控制错误率的检验框架。

Method: 将问题建模为最优控制问题，利用Bellman方程仅用时间和证据两个状态变量表示；对于硬截止情况，简化为有限时域Neyman-Pearson问题并找到最优e过程；对于指数衰减奖励，得到平稳近似并导出EDO准则。

Result: 1) 最优控制问题的Bellman表示仅需时间和证据；2) 硬截止下简单vs简单假设的最优e过程；3) 指数衰减奖励下的EDO准则，并在大时间尺度极限下恢复经典的GRO准则。

Conclusion: 该工作将时间偏好引入任意有效检验，EDO准则作为有限时间尺度上的最优准则，补充了传统GRO视角，为实际应用中平衡检验速度和统计效率提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Anytime-valid tests allow evidence to be checked during data collection: one can either continue testing or stop and reject the null while still controlling type-I error. Yet, in many applications rejection is useful only if it comes soon enough. We introduce a time-sensitive testing-by-betting framework that favours early rejection by assigning rewards to rejection times and maximising their expected value under a given alternative. This encompasses hard deadlines and softer time preferences. The resulting optimal control problem admits a Bellman representation in terms only of time and evidence against the null, rather than the full history. For hard deadlines, the simple-vs-simple case reduces to a finite-horizon Neyman--Pearson problem and identify the corresponding optimal e-process. Furthermore, we show that exponentially decaying rewards admit a stationary approximation, yielding the exponential-decay-optimal (EDO) criterion: a finite-time-scale counterpart to the classical growth-rate-optimal (GRO) viewpoint in anytime-valid statistics, with the GRO criterion recovered in the large-time-scale limit.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [GRALIS: A Unified Canonical Framework for Linear Attribution Methods via Riesz Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05480)
*Raimondo Fanale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRALIS是一个统一的数学框架，通过表示论证明了所有加性、线性和连续的归因泛函在L^2(Q,mu)上具有唯一的规范表示(Q, w, Delta)，涵盖了SHAP、IG、LIME和线性化GradCAM，提供了七项正式定理保证，在14条公理属性上达到13.5/14，远超单个方法的2.5-6/14，并在BreaKHis组织学图像上初步验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的XAI归因方法（GradCAM、SHAP、LIME、Integrated Gradients）建立在不同的理论基础之上，彼此之间缺乏形式化的可比性和统一的数学框架，导致在实际应用中难以选择合适的方法并理解它们之间的关系。

Method: GRALIS通过Riesz表示定理建立归因表示论，证明了每个加性、线性和连续的归因泛函在L^2(Q,mu)上都具有唯一的规范表示(Q, w, Delta)。该框架提供了七项正式定理保证：规范形式、精确完备性、蒙特卡洛收敛性、精确Shapley交互值、Hoeffding ANOVA分解、Sobol敏感性泛化以及多尺度扩展（MS-GRALIS）。

Result: 在BreaKHis数据集（1,187张组织学图像，DenseNet-121）上，GRALIS在删除忠实度方面恶性类别AUC提升+0.015，类别一致性达96%，SAL值为0.762±0.109，稀疏指数为0.39。在14条公理属性中满足13.5/14，远超单个方法的2.5-6/14。

Conclusion: GRALIS为XAI归因方法提供了统一的理论基础，同时满足完备性、敏感性、局部性、阶k交互和最优多尺度聚合等多项公理保证，有望成为可解释AI领域的基础性框架。更全面的基线方法对比将在后续论文中呈现。

Abstract: The main XAI attribution methods for deep neural networks -- GradCAM, SHAP, LIME, Integrated Gradients -- operate on separate theoretical foundations and are not formally comparable. We present GRALIS (Gradient-Riesz Averaged Locally-Integrated Shapley), a mathematical framework establishing a representation theory for attributions: every additive, linear, and continuous attribution functional on L^2(Q,mu) admits a unique canonical representation (Q, w, Delta), proved necessary by the Riesz Representation Theorem. This class encompasses SHAP, IG, LIME and linearized GradCAM, but excludes nonlinear functionals such as standard GradCAM or attention maps. Seven formal theorems provide simultaneous guarantees absent in any individual method: (T1) necessary canonical form; (T2) exact completeness; (T3) Monte Carlo convergence O(1/sqrt(m))+O(1/k); (T4) exact Shapley Interaction Values; (T5) Hoeffding ANOVA decomposition; (T6) Sobol sensitivity generalization; (T7) multi-scale extension (MS-GRALIS) with minimum-variance weights. An algebraic appendix justifies the GRALIS-SIV correspondence via the Mobius transform without circularity. GRALIS satisfies 13.5/14 axiomatic properties vs. 2.5-6/14 for individual methods, including completeness, sensitivity, locality, order-k interactions and optimal multi-scale aggregation simultaneously. Preliminary validation on BreaKHis (1,187 histology images, DenseNet-121) reports deletion faithfulness AUC +0.015 (malignant), 96% class-conditional consistency, SAL = 0.762+/-0.109 and sparsity index 0.39. Extended comparison with baseline XAI methods is planned for a companion paper.

</details>


### [260] [Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition via Pathwise Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05511)
*Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种非短视路径策略梯度方法(NM-PPG)，通过连续松弛和直通式展开策略，实现端到端优化主动特征获取问题，在合成和真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动特征获取(AFA)问题中特征获取成本高昂，现有方法存在方差高或短视策略的问题，需要一种能端到端优化非短视获取策略的方法。

Method: 将AFA建模为POMDP；引入连续松弛使获取过程可微，通过路径梯度避免高分差；提出直通式展开策略(前向硬获取、反向软松弛)；采用熵正则化和阶段性温度锐化稳定优化。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，NM-PPG的性能优于当前最先进的AFA基线方法。

Conclusion: NM-PPG通过非短视路径策略梯度有效解决了主动特征获取问题，为AFA提供了一种低方差、端到端可优化的新框架。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) considers prediction problems in which features are costly to obtain and the learner adaptively decides which feature values to acquire for each instance and when to stop and predict. AFA can be formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which naturally admits a sequential decision-making perspective. In this paper, we present non-myopic pathwise policy gradients (NM-PPG), a new AFA method built around this formulation. We introduce a continuous relaxation of the acquisition process that enables pathwise gradients through the full acquisition trajectory, avoiding the high variance of standard score-function policy gradients while allowing end-to-end optimization of a non-myopic acquisition policy. To better align training with deployment, we further develop a straight-through rollout scheme that follows hard feature acquisitions in the forward pass while backpropagating through the corresponding soft relaxation in the backward pass. We stabilize optimization with entropy regularization and staged temperature sharpening. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that NM-PPG yields superior performance relative to state-of-the-art AFA baselines.

</details>


### [261] [Bayesian Rain Field Reconstruction using Commercial Microwave Links and Diffusion Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05520)
*Badr Moufad,Albina Ilina,Hai Victor Habi,Salem Lahlou,Yazid Janati,Hagit Messer,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用扩散模型作为高保真空间先验，将降雨场重建视为贝叶斯逆问题，通过多种无训练后验采样方法（Plug-and-Play、序贯蒙特卡洛、副本交换）解决商用微波链路路径积分测量导致的地面降雨重建难题，在合成和真实数据集上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用微波链路（CMLs）在降雨传感中具有密集空间覆盖优势，但其路径积分测量特性使得准确的地面降雨重建十分困难。现有方法常将CMLs简化为点传感器，忽略了信号衰减与降雨之间的线积分关系，导致在非均匀降水条件下性能退化。

Method: 将降雨场重建建模为以扩散模型为高维空间先验的贝叶斯逆问题，利用扩散模型比审查高斯过程更好地保持关键降雨统计特性。通过Plug-and-Play、序贯蒙特卡洛和副本交换等多种无训练后验采样方法实现降雨场重建。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该方法在CML-based降雨重建任务中持续优于现有基线方法，扩散模型相比审查高斯过程能更好地保留降雨统计特征。

Conclusion: 扩散模型作为贝叶斯逆问题的先验，能够有效处理CML路径积分测量特性，提升非均匀降水条件下的降雨场重建精度，且支持多种无需额外训练的采样方法。

Abstract: Commercial Microwave Links (CMLs) offer dense spatial coverage for rainfall sensing but produce path-integrated measurements that make accurate ground-level reconstruction challenging. Existing methods typically oversimplify CMLs as point sensors and neglect line integration relating rainfall to signal attenuation, resulting in degraded performance under heterogeneous precipitation. In this work, we view rain field reconstruction as a Bayesian inverse problem with Diffusion Models (DMs) as high-fidelity spatial priors. We show that diffusion models better preserve key rainfall statistics compared to censored Gaussian processes. Framing rainfall estimation as a Bayesian inverse problem with a DM prior enables training-free posterior sampling using a broad family of methods, including Plug-and-Play, Sequential Monte Carlo, and Replica Exchange methods. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over established CML-based reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [262] [Optimal Contextual Pricing under Agnostic Non-Lipschitz Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05609)
*Jianyu Xu,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性估值和支撑集未知的有界噪声下的上下文动态定价问题，提出了Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$的最优遗憾，匹配下界并改进了先前$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$的上界，在非Lipschitz噪声场景下填补了长期存在的遗憾差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文动态定价算法依赖于需求曲线的光滑性进行跨上下文插值，但实际中的噪声可能具有非Lipschitz性质（任意跳跃和原子分布），导致传统方法失效。此前最优方法仅能达到$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$遗憾，与已知下界存在差距，亟需更优算法。

Method: 提出Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing算法，该算法结合了三个关键组件：随机化参数估计、保守残差网格探测（conservative residual-grid probing）以及基于置信度的一步重定向（confidence-based one-step redirection），整体运行时间为多项式时间。

Result: 算法实现了$\tilde O(T^{2/3})$的最优遗憾，匹配Kleinberg和Leighton（2003）已知的下界（仅差对数因子），并将Xu和Wang（2022）的$\tilde O(T^{3/4})$上界改进了$T^{1/12}$因子。

Conclusion: 在随机良态上下文条件下，本文提出的算法彻底解决了线性估值上下文动态定价在非Lipschitz噪声分布下的开放遗憾差距问题，实现了最优性。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing with linear valuations and bounded-support agnostic noise, whose induced demand curve may be non-Lipschitz with arbitrary jumps and atoms. Such discontinuities break the cross-context interpolation arguments used by smooth-demand pricing algorithms, while the best previous method achieved only $\tilde O(T^{3/4})$ regret. We propose Conservative-Markdown Redirect-UCB Pricing, a polynomial-time algorithm that combines randomized parameter estimation, conservative residual-grid probing, and confidence-based one-step redirection. Our algorithm achieves $\tilde O(T^{2/3})$ optimal regret, matching the known lower bounds of Kleinberg and Leighton (2003) up to logarithmic factors and improving over the previous upper bound of Xu and Wang (2022). Under stochastic well-conditioned contexts, this closes the long-existing open regret gap in linear-valuation contextual pricing under agnostic non-Lipschitz noise distribution.

</details>


### [263] [Sharper Guarantees for Misspecified Kernelized Bandit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05967)
*Davide Maran,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对核化老虎机优化中的错误指定（misspecification）问题，证明了对一大类核函数，错误指定的放大效应可从原来的 $\sqrt{d_\mathrm{eff}}$ 或 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 因子降低为对数或多项式对数增长。离线场景下，通过谱Lebesgue常数控制错误指定项；在线场景下，通过域分割算法去除了错误指定项中的额外 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 因子。核心原理是局部化（spectral localization 和 domain splitting）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核化老虎机优化的保证中，错误指定（misspecification）的代价与核复杂度（有效维度 $d_\mathrm{eff}$ 或最大信息增益 $\gamma_n$）的平方根相乘，导致严重的放大效应。本文旨在减少这种放大，使其仅呈对数或多项式对数增长。

Method: 离线场景：提出谱Lebesgue常数（spectral Lebesgue constant）来刻画近似算子的局部化性质，从而控制错误指定项的放大。在线场景：修改域分割（domain-splitting）算法，在温和的局部特征衰减假设下证明累积遗憾界，去除了错误指定项中的额外 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 因子。

Result: 离线场景：对一维单调谱得到对数放大，对多维傅里叶对角乘积核得到多项式对数放大。在线场景：在局部特征衰减假设下，得到 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{\gamma_n n}+n\varepsilon)$ 的累积遗憾界，消除了错误指定项中的 $\sqrt{\gamma_n}$ 因子。

Conclusion: 局部化（spectral localization 和 domain splitting）是降低错误指定放大效应的核心机制，能够将原本与核复杂度相乘的惩罚项缩减为对数或多项式对数级别。

Abstract: Existing guarantees for misspecified kernelized bandit optimization pay for misspecification through kernel complexity: in generic offline bounds, the misspecification level $\varepsilon$ is multiplied by $\sqrt{d_\mathrm{eff}}$, where $d_\mathrm{eff}$ is the kernel effective dimension, while in online regret bounds, the corresponding penalty is $\sqrt{γ_n}\,n\varepsilon$, where $γ_n$ is the maximum information gain after $n$ rounds of interaction.
  In this work, we show that, for a large class of kernels, the misspecification amplification can be reduced to logarithmic or polylogarithmic growth. In the offline setting, we first prove high-probability simple-regret bounds whose misspecification term is governed by a spectral Lebesgue constant. This yields logarithmic amplification for one-dimensional monotone spectra and polylogarithmic amplification for multivariate Fourier-diagonal product kernels. In the online setting, we modify a domain-splitting algorithm and prove a cumulative regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt{γ_n n}+n\varepsilon)$ under mild localized eigendecay assumptions, removing the extra $\sqrt{γ_n}$ factor from the misspecification term. The common principle is localization: spectral localization controls the Lebesgue constant of the offline approximation operator, while domain splitting implements the spatial analogue of this mechanism in the online setting, preventing local misspecification errors from being amplified globally.

</details>


### [264] [Bandit Learning in General Open Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06202)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究开放系统中的多臂老虎机学习问题，提出统一框架处理异构奖励和通用智能体模式，引入预训练度、稳定性和全局动态遗憾等新概念，并开发了具有可证明保证的全局UCB学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放系统老虎机研究对智能体到达和离开模式施加了严格的结构性假设，这些假设在实践中经常被违反，因此需要一种适用于通用开放系统的学习范式。

Method: 提出了统一开放系统老虎机问题框架，涵盖异构奖励和通用智能体模式；引入预训练度（量化新智能体进入时携带的信息量）、稳定性（衡量新智能体对系统的影响）和全局动态遗憾（比较所有活跃智能体的累积期望奖励与变化的最优臂）等概念；开发了全局UCB学习方法并给出可证明的遗憾界。

Result: 遗憾界表明：进入不确定性通过预训练度线性进入遗憾；在稳定系统中，遗憾由识别持久最优臂所需时间以及智能体模式共同决定；通过困难实例的下界证明了这些依赖关系的紧致性。

Conclusion: 本文为开放系统中的在线学习提供了统一的理论框架，揭示了智能体动态模式与学习性能之间的基本关系，为实际数字平台中的自适应决策提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital platforms have highlighted the prevalence of open systems, where agents can arrive and depart over time. While bandit learning in open systems has recently received initial attention, existing work imposes structural assumptions that are frequently violated in practice. A learning paradigm for general open systems creates fresh challenges: newly arriving agents induce endogenous non-stationarity; agent patterns determine how quickly information accumulates; and new agents make regret scale further with the time horizon. To this end, we formulate a unified open-system bandit problem with general dynamics, including heterogeneous rewards and general agent patterns. We introduce new concepts to capture the inherent complexities: the \emph{pre-training degree} of new agents quantifies how much information an agent carries upon entry, \emph{stability} measures the impact of new agents on the system, and \emph{global dynamic regret} compares the cumulative expected reward of all active agents with that of the varying optimal arms. We develop certified global-UCB learning methodologies with provable guarantees. Our regret bounds reveal that entry uncertainty enters linearly via the pre-training degree, while in stable regimes, regret is governed by the time needed to identify a persistent optimal arm, as well as by the agent patterns. We further show that these dependencies are tight via lower bounds in hard instances.

</details>


### [265] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出元博弈（metagame）框架，通过将归因方法视为合作博弈并计算其Shapley值，量化模型解释的二阶交互效应，理论证明归因可分层分解为元归因，并在指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器、多模态扩散Transformer等场景验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型解释方法（如Shapley值归因）仅关注一阶特征重要性，无法捕捉特征间的交互影响（即一个特征如何影响另一特征归因的定向效应）。需建立量化二阶交互效应的统一框架。

Method: 将归因方法ϕ(f)本身视为合作博弈，对特征j计算其对特征i归因的定向影响φ_{j→i}(f)，即元归因（meta-attribution）。通过Shapley值实现这一二阶归因计算，形成分层分解结构。

Result: 理论上证明归因可分层分解为元归因，且元归因是现有交互指标的定向扩展；实验上在三个场景验证：指令微调语言模型的词元交互、视觉-语言编码器的跨模态相似性解释、多模态扩散Transformer的文本到图像概念解释。

Conclusion: 元博弈框架为量化特征间的定向交互效应提供了理论严谨且实用的工具，拓展了可解释性分析从一阶归因到二阶交互的维度。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [266] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出缺失感知的顺序无关自回归模型（MO-ARM），将顺序无关自回归模型重新解释为缺失数据视角下的模型，实现在高缺失率下的鲁棒插补、在不完全数据上的直接训练以及主动信息获取，在多个真实基准上优于现有插补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序无关自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现优异，但其在不完整数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索。现有方法缺乏针对缺失数据的系统性框架。

Method: 1）证明标准训练在完全随机缺失机制下隐含执行插补；2）提出首个在不完全数据上直接训练的原则性框架，适用于一般缺失机制；3）利用摊销条件密度估计实现主动信息获取，顺序选择最具信息量的缺失变量。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准上，MO-ARM始终优于已有的插补基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过缺失数据视角重新审视顺序无关自回归模型，不仅揭示了其隐含的插补能力，还提出了可直接处理不完全数据的统一框架，并扩展了主动信息获取功能。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [267] [Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06474)
*Xiang Li,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-MMR框架，通过为每个数据点学习标量权重来近似目标策略下的期望回报，在仅需实现Q^π的条件下提供了数据依赖的有限样本保证，且误差维度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略评估方法在函数近似下缺乏有限样本保证，或需要复杂的统计复杂度条件，本文旨在建立一种更通用的、维度无关的OPE理论框架。

Method: Q-MMR通过矩匹配目标函数，以自顶向下归纳方式学习每个数据点的标量权重，使得重新加权后的奖励近似目标策略下的期望回报。该方法利用值函数判别器类进行矩匹配。

Result: 在仅需Q^π可实现的条件下，建立了数据依赖的有限样本保证，且误差界与函数类的统计复杂度无关（维度无关）；同时与重要性采样、线性FQE等现有方法建立了联系。

Conclusion: Q-MMR提供了一种全新的OPE理论视角，揭示了覆盖度（coverage）的本质，为离线强化学习提供了更通用的理论保证。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework, Q-MMR, for off-policy evaluation in finite-horizon MDPs. Q-MMR learns a set of scalar weights, one for each data point, such that the reweighted rewards approximate the expected return under the target policy. The weights are learned inductively in a top-down manner via a moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class. Notably, and perhaps surprisingly, a data-dependent finite-sample guarantee for general function approximation can be established under only the realizability of $Q^π$, with a dimension-free bound -- that is, the error does not depend on the statistical complexity of the function class. We also establish connections to several existing methods, such as importance sampling and linear FQE. Further theoretical analyses shed new light on the nature of coverage, a concept of fundamental importance to offline RL.

</details>


### [268] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MELO方法，通过记忆对冲的指数加权最小二乘在线聚合，在分布偏移下无需重训练即可自适应地组合多个基预测器，在法国电力负荷预测（含COVID-19封锁期）中相比基线降低34.7%的RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线预测中分布偏移普遍存在，预测器需要在平稳期保持稳定、在偏移时快速适应，但最优的适应记忆长度未知，现有方法难以在不重训练的情况下自动选择合适的遗忘尺度。

Method: MELO将任意非前瞻基预测器池与多个不同遗忘因子的EWLS自适应专家相结合，再通过参数免费的在线聚合规则MLpol聚合原始预测和EWLS适应后的预测，实现跨适应尺度的对冲。

Result: 在有界性条件下建立了确定性oracle不等式，证明其能与最优原始预测器及最优时变仿射组合竞争，跟踪代价与路径长度相关，聚合代价为次线性。在法国电力负荷预测（含COVID-19封锁）实验中，无需任何疫情指标或政策协变量，MELO相比仅用基预测器的MLpol降低RMSE 34.7%，并优于使用外部COVID政策协变量的TabICL参考方法。

Conclusion: MELO是一种轻量级、无需重训练的模型无关在线聚合方法，能有效应对分布偏移，兼具理论保证和实际性能优势。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [269] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究softmax注意力Transformer在线性分类数据上的上下文学习机制，构建了可执行上下文逻辑回归的多层Transformer，其中每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降。通过训练单层自注意力（由一步梯度下降监督）并循环堆叠得到循环模型，并提供了训练收敛性和分布外泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer在上下文学习中隐含执行特定算法的内在机制，特别是softmax注意力如何实现上下文学习。

Method: (1) 构建多层Transformer结构，每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降；(2) 通过单步梯度下降监督训练单层自注意力；(3) 循环堆叠训练好的单层得到循环模型；(4) 提供训练收敛性和分布外泛化保证的理论分析。

Result: 成功构造了可执行上下文逻辑回归的Transformer，证明了softmax注意力可以通过梯度下降机制实现有效的上下文学习，并给出了理论收敛性和泛化保证。

Conclusion: softmax Transformer通过模拟梯度下降算法实现上下文学习，为理解ICL机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [270] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型中注意力沉现象的结构根源：自注意力中的值聚合过程导致系统性方差差异，而FFN层的超神经元激活会放大这种差异，迫使模型形成注意力沉作为结构性锚点。通过两种干预实验验证了因果链，并提出head-wise RMSNorm架构修改以稳定预训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管注意力沉现象（初始token不成比例地占据注意力分数）在LLM中普遍存在，但其结构起源尚不明确，本文旨在提供该现象的机制性解释。

Method: 首先追溯注意力沉到自注意力中的值聚合过程导致的系统性方差差异，并证明FFN层中超神经元的激活会急剧放大此差异。通过两种受控干预验证因果链：(i)通过注意力掩码修改隔离聚合效应；(ii)放大目标token表示的方差。最后提出head-wise RMSNorm架构修改。

Result: 两种干预均能在任意位置复制注意力沉现象。提出的head-wise RMSNorm在预训练中稳定了值聚合输出，恢复了位置间的统计奇偶性，显著加速了收敛。

Conclusion: 本文提供了注意力沉的机制性解释，为系统控制沉形成奠定基础，并通过架构修改证明了恢复统计奇偶性可加速预训练收敛。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [271] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯递归投影校准（BRPC）框架，用于在线校准存在模拟器失配和非平稳性的流数据，通过分离无偏差粒子更新和条件高斯过程偏差更新处理渐进变化，并集成重启机制应对突变，在理论和实验上优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数-偏差混杂问题，且通常为离线过程，假设数据平稳生成，不适用于现代数字孪生中系统随时间演化、存在渐近漂移和突变的应用场景；数据同化方法虽支持顺序更新，但一般不显式建模系统偏差，对突变效果不佳。

Method: BRPC将投影校准扩展到在线设置：通过分离无偏差粒子更新（用于校准参数）和条件高斯过程更新（用于偏差），保持可识别性；集成重启机制检测突变并重置校准过程。

Result: 在合成数据和植物仿真基准上，BRPC在渐进变化下提高了校准精度；集成重启的BRPC在突变场景下进一步提升了鲁棒性和预测性能，优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线。

Conclusion: BRPC为数字孪生中的在线校准提供了偏差感知且可处理非平稳性的有效框架，具备理论保证和良好的实证表现。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [272] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习样例在预训练-微调范式下的行为，发现冻结预训练权重会削弱现有UE方法的效果，并提出分层欺骗策略SSC来保持数据不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UE方法主要在从头训练场景下评估，未考虑广泛使用的预训练-微调范式，导致其对实际隐私保护效果未知。

Method: 通过语义过滤机制分析发现，预训练冻结的浅层能保留数据语义并过滤非语义噪声；据此提出SSC（浅层语义伪装），在语义有效子空间中生成扰动以绕过预训练权重的语义抑制。

Result: 实验表明SSC在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性范式下仍能保持数据不可学习性，弥合了基于预训练的不可学习学习的关键差距。

Conclusion: 预训练-微调范式会显著削弱现有UE方法，而提出的SSC策略通过在语义子空间生成欺骗性扰动可有效应对这一挑战。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [273] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARDEN提出了一种基于f-散度模糊集分布鲁棒对抗训练框架，通过动态重加权对抗样本（KL散度下的log-sum-exp形式）来增强LLM鲁棒性，在保持模型效用和计算开销的同时显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对抗训练方法（如CAT、CAPO）计算昂贵且难以扩展，亟需一种更高效、可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法。

Method: 构建围绕经验训练分布的f-散度模糊集，通过凸对偶形式将目标函数转化为KL散度下的log-sum-exp形式，动态调整对抗样本权重以优化最坏情况下的对抗损失。

Result: 在多种LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低攻击成功率，计算代价和模型效用与CAT、CAPO、MixAT等基线相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN提供了一种实用且可扩展的鲁棒对齐方案，通过信息论目标有效平衡了鲁棒性与实用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [274] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在$f$-DP框架下对基于随机洗牌子采样的DP-SGD的权衡函数进行了紧致分析，推导出可解释的闭式界，并通过Berry-Esseen定理得到与理想随机猜测对角线仅差$\delta$的结果。对于单轮次，当$\delta=1/100$、$\sigma=1$时，约需$M\approx 1.14\times10^6$轮和$N\approx 1.14\times10^7$训练样本。多轮次组合下$\delta$具有线性依赖关系，限制了$E=O(\sqrt{M})$。进一步引入基于大数定律推广的新证明技术，得到了渐近随机猜测对角线极限结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Poisson子采样的$f$-DP分析虽然可机器计算，但公式隐式不透明；而随机洗牌子采样缺乏紧致的闭式分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供透明可解释的隐私分析界。

Method: 在$f$-DP框架下分析随机洗牌子采样的DP-SGD权衡函数，利用Berry-Esseen定理推导紧致闭式界；引入基于大数定律推广的新证明技术处理渐近极限情况。

Result: 推导出单轮次下与理想随机猜测对角线仅差$\delta$的紧致界；多轮次组合下$\delta$具有$O(\sqrt{E})$依赖关系；给出了具体参数设置示例。

Conclusion: 随机洗牌子采样允许透明闭式隐私分析，与Poisson子采样形成互补；渐近极限结果的显式收敛速率仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [275] [PACZero: PAC-Private Fine-Tuning of Language Models via Sign Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06505)
*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Xiaochen Zhu,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Marten van Dijk,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACZero提出了一种PAC隐私的零阶微调机制，在严格保证互信息为零（I=0）的条件下，通过符号量化子集聚合梯度实现成员推断攻击抵抗，并在SST-2和SQuAD上取得了有竞争力的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP框架在强隐私保护（ε<1）下无法产生可用效用，而PAC隐私框架允许在成员推断攻击后验成功率不高于先验的条件下，通过释放条件互信息为零的梯度符号来实现更优的隐私-效用权衡。

Method: 提出两种变体：PACZero-MI（通过二元释放的精确校准实现预算化互信息）和PACZero-ZPL（在分歧步骤使用均匀随机掷硬币实现I=0）。核心思想是符号量化子集聚合的零阶梯度，当所有候选子集对更新方向达成一致时，释放梯度符号的条件互信息为零。

Result: 在SST-2 OPT-1.3B全参数微调I=0条件下，PACZero-ZPL达到88.99±0.91，仅比非私有MeZO基线低2.1个百分点。在OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B上均取得有竞争力的准确率和F1值。

Conclusion: PACZero首次在严格互信息为零的高隐私保护水平下实现了可用效用，超越了传统DP框架在该隐私区域的性能极限。

Abstract: We introduce PACZero, a family of PAC-private zeroth-order mechanisms for fine-tuning large language models that delivers usable utility at $I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$. This privacy regime bounds the membership-inference attack (MIA) posterior success rate at the prior, an MIA-resistance level the DP framework matches only at $\varepsilon=0$ and infinite noise. All DP-ZO comparisons below are matched at the MIA posterior level. The key insight is that PAC Privacy charges mutual information only when the release depends on which candidate subset is the secret. Sign-quantizing subset-aggregated zeroth-order gradients creates frequent unanimity, steps at which every candidate subset agrees on the update direction; at these steps the released sign costs zero conditional mutual information. We propose two variants that span the privacy-utility trade-off: PACZero-MI (budgeted MI via exact calibration on the binary release) and PACZero-ZPL ($I=0$ via a uniform coin flip on disagreement steps). We evaluate on SST-2 and SQuAD with OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B in both LoRA and full-parameter tracks. On SST-2 OPT-1.3B full fine-tuning at $I=0$, PACZero-ZPL reaches ${88.99\pm0.91}$, within $2.1$pp of the non-private MeZO baseline ($91.1$ FT). No prior method produces usable utility in the high-privacy regime $\varepsilon<1$, and PACZero-ZPL obtains competitive SST-2 accuracy and nontrivial SQuAD F1 across OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B at $I=0$.

</details>


### [276] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用《All of Us》全国纵向EHR数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口统计学分层模型，实现了对慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）的早期预测（AUC=0.846），优于基线方法0.0168，展示了常规EHR数据在CRS风险分层中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRS是一种常见且异质性高的炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，症状与过敏性鼻炎等重叠，且现有预测研究多依赖单中心队列，缺乏人群普适性。因此，需要利用大规模、全国代表性的EHR数据来建立更通用的CRS早期预测模型。

Method: 利用《All of Us》研究项目的全国纵向EHR数据，提取诊断前两年的病史；采用混合特征选择管道（结合基于患病率的统计筛选和基于模型的重要性排序），将约11万个候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征；针对六个人口统计学亚组（不同性别和生命阶段）训练分层模型，并进行亚组特异性超参数调优。

Result: 整体AUC达到0.8461，较最佳基线方法提高了0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据能够支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，有助于在初级保健中实现更早的分诊和转诊优先级排序。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [277] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT)，一种无需协调器的多智能体训练范式。SAT将智能体团队表示为因子化策略，采用块坐标更新实现去中心化训练，并设计了序列感知、基于当前策略的advantage估计器以及每个智能体的KL信任域。理论上有单调改进保证和可证明的即插即用不变性。实验表明，三个4B智能体（共12B）在AIME24/25上超越Qwen3-32B 3.9%，且将两个智能体替换为8B版本后性能提升10.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多个小模型组成的智能体团队在联合训练时出现的复合分布偏移问题，以及训练过程中的协调和稳定性困难。

Method: SAT将团队策略建模为因子化策略，采用无需中央控制器的块坐标更新方式对智能体进行逐步更新。具体包括：序列感知的、基于当前团队策略的on-policy advantage估计器，以及每个智能体独立的KL信任域约束，以隔离占位漂移。

Result: 三个4B参数智能体（总计12B）在AIME24/25基准上平均超越Qwen3-32B 3.9%。将两个智能体替换为8B版本后，综合得分提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT框架提供了单调改进和即插即用不变性的理论保证，使得团队中任意智能体可被升级为更强模型而无需重新训练整个团队，且性能边界可证明提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [278] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了horizon-constrained Rashomon sets框架，首次建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的严格联系，证明了有效Rashomon集随预测时间以Lyapunov指数速率指数收缩，并开发了基于决策效用的模型选择算法，在混沌系统中将决策质量提升18-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（predictive multiplicity）和混沌动力学（chaotic dynamics）是机器学习中两个独立发展的基本挑战，尽管它们存在概念上的联系。现有研究缺乏将二者统一的理论框架，特别是在安全关键混沌系统中，模型多样性如何随时间演化这一问题尚未被探讨。

Method: 1) 提出horizon-constrained Rashomon sets理论框架，刻画混沌系统中模型多重性随预测时间尺度的演化；2) 证明有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数速率随前导时间指数收缩；3) 引入Lyapunov加权度量，提供预测分歧的更紧界；4) 开发基于下游效用（而非单纯预测精度）的决策对齐模型选择算法。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky合成混沌系统以及风电、交通、天气等真实应用中，所提出框架将决策质量提升18-34%，同时保持有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首个严格理论联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域部署机器学习提供了有原则的指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [279] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了概念操控的理论框架，证明标准操控方法是LEACE的特例，提出LEACE-Switch用于概念切换，并引入更通用的MidSteer方法，在多个任务和架构上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念操控（Steering）在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全设置中取得了实证成功，但缺乏全面的理论框架，本文旨在弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 1. 建立操控与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）之间的理论联系；2. 提出LEACE-Switch作为概念切换的理论框架并刻画其最优性条件；3. 引入MidSteer（最小扰动概念操控），一种更通用的仿射框架，放松假设条件并支持有向的最小扰动变换。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 通过形式化概念操控的理论基础，证明了现有方法（LEACE）与其关联，并提出了更通用的MidSteer框架，为生成模型的可控生成提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [280] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种可学习的逐token门控机制，通过在连续Transformer块间对残差更新施加门控，让模型根据每个token的上下文难度动态决定是否跳过某些层。TSA使用轻量级两层MLP生成连续的停止概率，端到端可微，仅增加1.7%参数，无需改动基础架构。实验表明，即便在无深度正则化的情况下，任务损失梯度本身就能驱动路由器跳过20%的token-层操作。在字符级语言建模任务中，TSA以<0.5%的质量损失节省14-23%的计算量，在同等效率下比早退方法验证损失低0.7%，且学习到的路由可直接用于推理时的稀疏执行以实现实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token应用相同数量的层，忽视了不同token在上下文难度上的差异，导致计算效率低下。作者希望设计一种机制，让模型能够根据每个token的难度动态决定计算深度，从而在不显著牺牲质量的前提下提升效率。

Method: TSA在每两个连续Transformer块之间插入一个轻量级门控模块（两层MLP），为每个token生成一个连续的停止概率（介于0到1之间），乘以残差更新后再传递给下一层。该机制端到端可微，无需强化学习或离散决策。训练时使用基于期望计算量的正则化项（λ控制强度），但实验发现即使λ=0时路由器也能学会跳过约20%的操作。

Result: 在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8数据集上，TSA以<0.5%的验证损失增加节省了14-23%的token-层操作（TLOps）。在同等计算效率下，TSA的验证损失比早退（early exit）方法低0.7%。学习到的路由策略可以直接应用于推理时的稀疏执行，实现真实的端到端加速。

Conclusion: TSA证明了一种简单、轻量且可微的逐token动态深度路由方法是有效的。它无需架构改动，参数开销极小，且路由器能够在无显式深度正则化信号的情况下自主学会难度自适应的跳过策略。该方法在字符级语言建模上取得有竞争力的效率-质量权衡，并为动态计算在Transformer中的更广泛应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [281] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何框架分析稀疏自编码器中特征组合的不稳定性，揭示了高维稀疏锥流形中组合塌缩阈值的存在，并发现ReLU整流在高压制区间会产生类似棘轮效应的干扰累积，验证了CLEVR数据集上的预测趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管稀疏自编码器（SAE）在解耦特征叠加和实现激活控制方面表现出色，但组合式激活控制（同时激活多个语义潜变量）的理论基础尚不明确。线性表示假说忽略了过完备字典中存在的非线性干扰效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球面字典模型下推导组合塌缩阈值（由信号锥的高斯均值宽度/统计维度刻画）；分析高偏置区间ReLU整流如何将微观相关性方差波动转化为系统性漂移，产生类似棘轮效应（ratchet effect）的干扰增长。

Result: 在CLEVR数据集的结构化语义特征上验证了预测的缩放趋势，发现层级相关性会加速塌缩转换，相对于随机基线表现更明显。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了基于组合的激活控制在可扩展性方面受到几何约束，需要显式管理干扰的合成机制，而非依赖朴素线性叠加。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [282] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中的效率瓶颈（拖后腿效应），提出训练无关的MACS框架，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量机制，分别解决视觉token的信息异质性和模态动态性导致的资源错配问题，显著提升多模态基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中因拖后腿效应存在严重效率瓶颈，而多模态场景下现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法解决两个挑战：信息异质性（冗余视觉token与关键token被同等对待）和模态动态性（不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配）。

Method: 提出MACS训练无关推理框架：(1) 熵加权负载机制，量化视觉token的语义价值以解决信息异质性；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制，根据输入实时模态组成分配专家资源。

Result: 在多模态基准上显著优于现有方法，提供鲁棒的MoE MLLMs高效部署方案。

Conclusion: MACS通过模态感知的容量缩放，在不需重新训练的情况下有效解决了MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中的效率问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [283] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将结果监督内化为过程监督的新方法，使模型在仅有结果奖励的情况下，通过识别、纠正和重用失败的推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号，实现更细粒度的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习推理方法面临两难：结果级奖励难以进行精确的信用分配，而外部过程监督成本高昂且难以持续扩展。核心挑战在于如何将序列末端的反馈转化为可指导中间推理步骤的细粒度学习信号。

Method: 提出监督内化方法，将强化学习中的推理问题重新理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的过程。模型通过自动识别、纠正和重用失败的推理轨迹，提取过程级学习信号，在仅有结果监督的条件下实现更细粒度的策略优化。这一思想进一步被抽象为一种新的训练范式。

Result: 该方法能够在仅有结果监督的条件下，实现比传统结果级优化更精细的信用分配，避免了对外部过程标注的依赖，为可扩展的推理强化学习提供了新路径。

Conclusion: 强化学习推理的核心不在于结果奖励的稀疏性本身，而在于如何内化外部反馈为过程监督信号。本文提出的监督内化范式开辟了不同于外部过程监督的细粒度信用分配新方向。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [284] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT，一种在线数据重加权框架，通过在训练过程中动态调整每个样本的学习率（基于相似度质量信号），替代传统离线数据筛选/混合方法。在指令微调和大规模预训练中，ADAPT在相同FLOPs下显著优于离线方法和先前的在线方法，实现更强的跨基准泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据策展方法（如数据筛选和混合）采用离线范式，与训练过程分离，导致工程开销大、在模型/任务变化时需重新运行整个流程，且通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据量会牺牲多样性，损害模型泛化能力。

Method: 将数据策展重新定义为在线重加权问题，提出ADAPT框架：通过基于相似度的质量信号为每个训练样本动态调整自适应学习率，在不改变样本数量的情况下实现训练过程中的损失加权，作为一种隐式课程学习器，随着模型演化逐步从粗粒度模式转向细粒度语义区分。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验中，ADAPT在同等FLOPs下一致优于离线数据筛选/混合方法以及先前的在线方法，展现出更强的跨基准泛化性能。

Conclusion: 在线动态重加权范式是传统离线数据策展的有效替代方案，ADAPT通过自适应学习率机制实现了更优的泛化能力，为LLM数据策展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [285] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用进化策略优化预训练量化模型的权重分配，通过迭代微调少量权重的量化状态，显著提升了量化模型的精度，并在VGG、ResNet和自编码器上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备、实时系统中受限于复杂度和内存大小，量化是一种主流压缩技术，但基于最近邻取整的量化方式不能保证最优精度，因此需要更高效的量化优化方法。

Method: 采用进化策略（evolution strategy）作为优化方法，每轮迭代只改变少量权重的值，将其移动到不同的量化状态，通过合适的算子与参数设置逐步提升模型精度。

Result: 在VGG、ResNet等图像分类与检测架构以及自编码器上的实验表明，所提进化策略能够快速提升量化模型的精度。

Conclusion: 基于进化策略的量化状态优化方法能够有效克服最近邻取整的局限性，是一种有潜力的量化模型精度提升方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [286] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过估计每层参数的任务重要性分数来调节梯度更新，缓解LLM在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法（数据回放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对LLM内部知识分布的语义感知能力，无法区分哪些参数应该保留或更新，导致灾难性遗忘问题未能有效解决。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架，在每个Transformer层中估计逐元素的任务特定参数重要性分数，利用这些分数调节梯度更新：对先前任务重要的参数更新幅度小，不重要的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试中，该方法在旧任务保留和新任务学习上均一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 归因引导的参数重要性估计能有效区分LLM中需要保留和更新的参数，在缓解灾难性遗忘的同时保持对新任务的学习能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [287] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CopyCop算法，能在对抗性变换下识别出模仿其他GNN输出的复制猫GNN，提供理论保证，并在14个数据集和5种架构上验证了准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印和指纹方法无法在对抗性变换、不同架构和嵌入维度下识别复制猫GNN，需要一种更强大的所有权验证方法。

Method: 提出CopyCop算法，能够在两个GNN具有不同架构、权重和嵌入维度，且对抗方可对输出嵌入进行变换的条件下，识别出复制猫GNN。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop准确且对广泛的对抗攻击和变换具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CopyCop能够有效识别复制猫GNN，克服了现有方法的局限性，并提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [288] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新型配体选择算法，仅需平均40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上中位数提升7%-32%，且比最接近的竞争对手快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中，候选配体通过早期筛选的比例不足5%，且需要对未知蛋白也能有效工作，因此需要一种样本高效、可迭代的配体选择方法。

Method: 提出SPADE算法，采用新型配体选择策略，通过从稀疏数据中学习，实现高效迭代测试与选择。

Result: SPADE平均只需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体；在逐项对比中，SPADE在更多蛋白上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%，同时打分速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，为药物发现中配体筛选提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [289] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用预训练扩散模型进行零样本条件采样，解决线性逆问题（如修补和超分辨率）。通过分数函数的法向-切向分解，证明无条件切向分数替换条件切向分数的误差上界由条件互信息决定，并提出投影朗之万初始化结合引导反向去噪的方法，在实验中优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于投影的采样器仅强制测量一致性，但未能正确分配可行集上的概率质量，导致条件采样存在偏差。需要从理论上理解偏差来源并设计无偏采样方法。

Method: 对分数函数进行法向-切向分解：观测方向分数由测量精确确定，仅切向条件分数未知。理论证明无偏切向分数替换误差上界由条件互信息（观测与未观测分量之间）控制，分解为初始化和路径分数失配误差。提出投影-朗之万初始化结合引导反向去噪。

Result: 在修补和超分辨率实验中，所提方法优于强投影基线方法。

Conclusion: 为条件扩散采样提供了信息论视角的误差分析，法向-切向分解揭示了偏差来源，所提初始化加引导方法有效改善了条件采样质量。

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [290] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将路由策略拆分为节点排列和边选择两个阶段，通过预编码边聚合方案、非自回归架构和分层强化学习，高效解决多图车辆路径问题（VRP）中的可扩展性问题，在六个VRP变体上匹配或超越现有最优方法，且训练和推理速度显著更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法大多局限于欧几里得设置或简单图，难以处理具有平行边（代表不同旅行选项）的多图VRP，而少数针对多图的方法存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出NEPF方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；引入预编码边聚合方案和非自回归架构用于边阶段，以及分层强化学习方法以联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上匹配或超越现有最优方法的解质量，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。
