Result: 大量实验表明，UAT-MC在防御-准确率权衡下显著提升了对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持了可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: 本文首次识别了多模态推荐系统中逃避式推广攻击下的跨模态梯度失配现象，并通过UAT-MC实现了有效的鲁棒防御，为多模态推荐系统的安全性研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [363] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层的校准诊断协议，用于区分图语言模型是对训练图的结构规律进行学习还是单纯记忆。实验表明，大规模图语言模型（如LLaMA风格模型）在高频区域能够习得结构规律，但在小规模下其表现与记忆基线难以区分；高频模式被良好复现，而稀有模式覆盖率低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚合保真度指标无法区分图语言模型是学习了结构规律还是仅仅记忆了训练图，亟需一种能够解开记忆与结构对齐的诊断方法。

Method: 开发了一种校准诊断协议，包含三个组件：频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线、三级频率分层。通过在五个TU基准数据集上评估LLaMA风格的图语言模型，结合两种图序列化方式（规范DFS码和动作序列）进行验证。

Result: 大规模（375万图）下，逐字记忆急剧下降而秩相关保持高位；固定子样本分析中，仅新子集的频繁子图挖掘与全生成Spearman相关性高度一致；高频模式在所有规模下得到良好复现，稀有模式覆盖率低且随容量增加改善有限。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在大规模下能够学习超越记忆的结构规律（主要在高频区域），但诊断协议揭示了其在小规模下与记忆难以区分的本质，以及高频-低频覆盖的不对称性。该结论在两种图序列化方式下具有稳健性。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [364] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了前向-前向（FF）训练中存在的“层搭便车”现象：后续层会依赖前面层已经建立的分类边界，导致深层梯度随正裕度积累而指数衰减。提出了三种局部修复方法（逐块、硬门控、深度缩放），在CIFAR-10/100上可将深层分离统计提升4-45倍，但对最终精度影响不足1个百分点。实验表明，架构和数据增强对精度的主导作用远大于训练规则修改，说明层搭便车虽是真实且可修复的优化病态，但并非限制FF训练精度的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在累积好度（cumulative-goodness）的前向-前向训练中，后续层会“继承”前面层已经部分完成的任务，导致深层梯度信号衰减，形成层搭便车问题。作者旨在形式化这一病态并提出局部修复方法，同时验证该问题是否真正限制了FF训练的最终精度。

Method: 首先从理论上形式化了softplus FF准则下类判别梯度随前面块积累的正裕度指数衰减的规律。然后提出三种局部修复策略：逐块（per-block）分离训练、硬门控（hardness-gated）选择、深度缩放（depth-scaled）梯度补偿，均不依赖反向传播梯度。在CIFAR-10/100上进行实验，并在Tiny ImageNet上验证跨数据集泛化性，同时进行校准实验分离架构/增强与训练规则的影响。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100上，三种修复方法使深层层的分离统计量提升4-45倍，但对非退化训练流程的精度变化不足1个百分点。Tiny ImageNet上呈现相同的定性差距：层健康诊断改善但最终精度不受影响。校准实验表明架构和数据增强对最终精度的影响远大于训练规则修改。

Conclusion: 累积层搭便车是FF训练中真实存在且可修复的优化病态，但就本文所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集而言，它并非限制可达精度的主导因素。精度瓶颈更多来自架构和数据增强等其他因素。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [365] [Structure-Preserving Gaussian Processes Via Discrete Euler-Lagrange Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06246)
*Jan-Hendrik Ewering,Kathrin Flaßkamp,Niklas Wahlström,Thomas B. Schön,Thomas Seel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拉格朗日高斯过程（LGPs），通过离散受控欧拉-拉格朗日方程实现概率化、数据高效的动力学学习，在无外力时保持几何结构，仅从离散位置快照即可学习物理一致的动力学模型，实现稳定长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学学习方法通常需要速度或动量信息，但在运动捕捉、视觉伺服等实际场景中往往只能获取位置测量数据。此外，传统方法难以保证物理一致性，导致长期预测中出现能量漂移问题。

Method: 基于拉格朗日-达朗贝尔原理的几何结构，利用离散受控欧拉-拉格朗日方程和变分离散化方案构造高斯过程条件化的线性算子，仅从离散位置快照学习动力学模型。

Result: 在多种合成与真实案例（包括具有滞回特性的软体机器人）中，LGPs仅从稀疏位置数据即可学习物理一致的动力学，提供不确定性量化，实现稳定的长期预测，展现出数据高效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: LGPs通过嵌入物理几何结构，克服了能量漂移问题，为仅有位置测量数据的实际应用（如运动捕捉、视觉伺服）提供了一种概率化、数据高效且物理一致的动力学学习方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Lagrangian Gaussian Processes (LGPs) for probabilistic and data-efficient learning of dynamics via discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations. Importantly, the geometric structure of the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle, which governs the motion of dynamical systems, is preserved by construction in the absence of external forces. This allows learning physically consistent models that overcome erroneous drift in the system's energy, thereby providing stable long-term predictions. At the core of our approach lie linear operators for Gaussian process conditioning, constructed from discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations and variational discretization schemes. Thereby and unlike prior work, the method enables learning dynamics from discrete position snapshots, i.e., without access to a system's velocities or momenta. This is particularly relevant for a large class of practical scenarios where only position measurements are available, for instance, in motion capture or visual servoing applications. We demonstrate the data-efficiency and generalization capabilities of the LGPs in various synthetic and real-world case studies, including a real-world soft robot with hysteresis. The experimental results underscore that the LGPs learn physically consistent dynamics with uncertainty quantification solely from sparse positional data and enable stable long-term predictions.

</details>


### [366] [The Role of Node Features in Graph Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06250)
*Jan von Pichowski,Alžbeta Hrabošová,Ingo Scholtes,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图池化在图分类中效果不稳定的原因，发现池化操作需要节点特征与图拓扑结构良好对齐，并提出了节点特征质量的量化指标和有效池化的基本要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图池化在图分类中相比标准WL-1表达力的GNN往往只有边际或不一致的提升，这一差距的根源未被充分理解。

Method: 通过分析节点特征与图拓扑结构的交互作用对池化目标的影响，形式化定义了有效池化对节点特征的基本要求，并引入特征质量的定量度量。

Result: 当节点特征满足与图拓扑结构良好对齐的条件时，池化能够带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 池化操作的有效性依赖于节点特征与图拓扑的对齐程度，满足该条件时池化在适当数据集上可发挥积极作用。

Abstract: Graph pooling is commonly applied in graph classification, yet its empirical gains over standard WL-1 expressive GNNs are often marginal or inconsistent. We study this gap by analysing the interaction between node features and graph topology and their effect on pooling objectives. Our analysis reveals that pooling operators require node features that are well-aligned with the graph's topology -- a condition often overlooked and not guaranteed in empirical networks. We formalise fundamental requirements for node features to enable effective pooling, and introduce a quantitative measure of feature quality. Our empirical evaluation shows that, when these requirements are satisfied, pooling can be beneficial and improve performance on appropriate datasets.

</details>


### [367] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦图流形校准（FedGMC），通过双流形校准机制从统一流形视角解决图联邦学习中的语义异构和结构异构问题，在11个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图联邦学习方法通过强制对齐模型参数或原型来处理异构性，但隐式依赖全局线性假设，压缩了客户端的个性化表示空间，无法保留多样化的局部图分布。

Method: 提出FedGMC：1) 语义异构方面，服务器通过等距语义锚构建几何最优语义流形，引导局部语义流形校准；2) 结构异构方面，服务器通过全局结构模板构建全局结构流形，引导局部结构流形校准；3) 服务器通过聚合局部流形动态精炼全局语义流形和结构流形。

Result: 在11个同配性和异配性图数据集上的大量实验表明，FedGMC有效平衡了全局共性和局部个性化，显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC从统一流形视角统一处理语义异构和结构异构，通过双流形校准机制在保持全局共性的同时最大化局部个性化表示空间，为图联邦学习的异构性问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [368] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向端到端自动驾驶规划的层次化归因框架，通过粗到细的区域归因策略从六视角输入中定位关键视觉证据，并提取三种归因统计量（熵、空间方差、基尼系数）作为规划风险的预测信号。实验表明这些统计量与轨迹误差和碰撞检测显著相关，且具有良好的泛化性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型将多视角输入直接映射为未来轨迹，提升了系统集成度，但存在决策不透明、风险难以定位的问题。现有方法依赖辅助监控模型或生成文本解释，但这些方法与规划过程解耦，无法揭示轨迹生成背后的视觉证据。归因方法虽可直接解释模型，但规划任务与图像分类不同，需处理六视角输入并预测连续多步轨迹，要求归因同时捕获关键视角、区域及其对输出的影响。此外，归因图能否支持风险识别尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出层次化归因框架：以原始轨迹的L2一致性为目标，设计粗到细的区域归因策略，先在完整六视角输入中搜索候选区域，再在区域内精细化归因。进一步提取三种归因统计量作为规划风险的预测信号：归因熵（衡量规划器对联合视觉空间的依赖集中程度）、视角内空间方差（表征各视角内归因的分散程度）、跨视角基尼系数（量化归因在六个相机间分布的不均匀性）。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD和GenAD三个模型上的实验表明，所提统计量与轨迹误差的Spearman相关系数达0.30±0.07，碰撞检测的AUROC达0.77±0.04。该信号能泛化到未见场景且性能几乎无下降，并在替代归因基线下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的层次化归因框架能够有效揭示端到端规划模型的视觉证据，提取的归因统计量可作为规划风险的可靠预测信号，为自动驾驶的安全性和可解释性提供了新的工具。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [369] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出函数投影流匹配（FP-FM）算法，通过学习基函数跨越训练分布的速场，利用最小二乘投影实现对新目标分布的无额外训练高效生成，在合成和图像数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在自然语言条件生成任务上取得显著成功，但从示例数据点实现模型自适应仍然是一个探索不足且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: FP-FM学习基函数以张成对应一组训练分布的速场，通过计算简单的最小二乘投影来适应新分布，并引入多种变体（如使系数依赖时间）以在表达能力和计算量之间进行权衡。

Result: 在合成数据集和基于图像的数据集上，FP-FM在精确率和召回率方面均大幅优于基线方法，尤其是在未见过的分布上表现强劲。

Conclusion: FP-FM能够高效地从多样化的目标分布中生成样本，推理时无需额外训练，为基于示例的自适应生成建模提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [370] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层次感知交叉熵（HACE），通过预测聚合和祖先标签平滑将类别层次结构融入损失函数，在多个数据集和架构上显著优于标准交叉熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵将所有误分类等同对待，忽略了类别层次编码中的语义距离信息。

Method: HACE包含两个组件：预测聚合（将模型概率质量沿层次向上传播）和祖先标签平滑（将真实标签信号沿路径从真实类别分布到根节点）。

Result: 端到端训练中，18个架构-数据集组合中15个提升准确率，平均增益4.66%；线性探测中在所有三个数据集上超越所有对比方法，平均提升2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE作为标准交叉熵的即插即用替代品，能有效利用层次结构信息提升分类性能。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [371] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度检查点的内存高效全梯度评估框架，用于对迭代随机净化防御进行白盒对抗攻击评估，证明精确梯度计算比近似梯度能发现更多漏洞，从而避免高估模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对扩散和Langevin净化防御的鲁棒性评估常因内存限制而使用近似反向传播，这会削弱攻击信号并高估鲁棒性；同时，迭代净化过程中的随机性也未被有效控制，导致鲁棒性指标不稳定。

Method: 利用梯度检查点技术实现精确端到端梯度计算（以额外重计算换取低内存占用），结合控制随机性的评估协议，构建全梯度白盒攻击框架。

Result: 在扩散净化与基于EBM的Langevin采样上，全梯度攻击发现了近似梯度评估遗漏的漏洞，实现了更强的$\ell_{\infty}$和$\ell_{2}$白盒攻击，并能探测分布外鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确梯度评估对于迭代随机防御的可靠基准测试至关重要，近似方法会导致鲁棒性被高估。

Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations. Our framework yields stronger state-of-the-art $\ell_{\infty}$ and $\ell_{2}$ white-box attacks and further supports probing out-of-distribution robustness. Overall, our results show that exact-gradient evaluation is essential for reliable benchmarking of iterative stochastic defenses.

</details>


### [372] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pro-KLShampoo优化器，通过利用KL-Shampoo克罗内克预条件子的"尖峰-平坦"谱结构，在低秩子空间保留全谱信息，其余方向进行正交化，在多个规模的语言模型预训练中优于KL-Shampoo，同时降低内存和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL-Shampoo等矩阵结构优化器与基于梯度动量正交化的方法（如Muon）通常各自独立发展，缺乏融合。作者观察到KL-Shampoo的克罗内克因子呈现"尖峰-平坦"谱形状（少数主导特征值+近似均匀尾部），这一结构可用于设计更高效的优化器。

Method: 1) 观察到KL-Shampoo预条件子的克罗内克因子具有"尖峰-平坦"谱结构，并在秩-ρ信号加噪声梯度模型下严格成立。2) 将其中一个克罗内克因子限制为参数化族：在跟踪的r维子空间保留完整谱结构，其余n-r方向共享单一特征值并在这些方向应用正交化。3) 通过恒等式证明该正交化恢复了完整KL-Shampoo预条件子的代数形式。

Result: 在GPT-2 124M/350M、LLaMA 134M/450M四个预训练规模上，Pro-KLShampoo在各个子空间秩下均在验证损失、每GPU峰值内存和达到各损失水平的墙钟时间上持续优于KL-Shampoo。

Conclusion: 通过利用预条件子的谱结构，可以在保持KL-Shampoo性能的同时显著降低计算和内存开销，为大规模LLM训练提供更高效的优化方案。

Abstract: Optimizers that exploit the matrix structure of gradients are central to modern LLM pre-training, with two distinct frontiers: explicit Kronecker-factored preconditioning -- most recently KL-Shampoo, which estimates the preconditioner via KL divergence minimization -- and orthogonalization of the gradient momentum, exemplified by Muon and analyzed as steepest descent under the spectral norm. The two routes are typically developed in isolation. We make a structural observation about KL-Shampoo's Kronecker preconditioners: their eigenvalue spectra exhibit a \emph{spike-and-flat} shape -- a few dominant eigenvalues followed by an approximately uniform tail -- across layers and training stages, holding exactly under a rank-$ρ$ signal-plus-noise gradient model. We exploit this structure by restricting one of KL-Shampoo's Kronecker factors to a parametric family aligned with the spike-and-flat shape: full spectral structure on a tracked $r$-dimensional subspace, single shared eigenvalue across the remaining $n-r$ directions. On these directions, we apply orthogonalization. An identity shows that this orthogonalization recovers the algebraic form of full KL-Shampoo's preconditioner. On four pre-training scales (GPT-2 124M / 350M, LLaMA 134M / 450M), Pro-KLShampoo consistently outperforms KL-Shampoo at every subspace rank we test in validation loss, peak per-GPU memory, and wallclock time to reach each loss level.

</details>


### [373] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE是一个两阶段框架，通过先主动生成多样化学习器再修剪冗余学习器的方式，在保证对原始集成的忠实度的同时，实现比现有剪枝和压缩方法更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型虽然性能优异，但需要聚合大量弱学习器，这阻碍了部署、可解释性和鲁棒性验证等下游任务。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段通过理论指导的过程主动生成新学习器以增强初始集成的多样性；第二阶段在无更多相关学习器可生成时，对 enriched ensemble 进行剪枝。两个阶段均可精细控制对原始集成的忠实度。

Result: 实验表明PACE在性能上优于先前的剪枝和压缩方法，同时提供有原则的忠实度保证控制。

Conclusion: PACE通过交织生成与剪枝两种范式，实现了比单一范式更优的集成模型精简效果，并提供了可量化的忠实度保障。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [374] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，用于预测MoE模型是否会产生死专家（dead experts）。通过12个对照实验（8个视觉、4个语言任务，总计超过11,000个训练轮次），证明当E >= 0.5时可保证零死专家，无需手动设计的负载均衡辅助损失函数。此外还发现了六项额外结论，包括死专家可复苏、正交毒性具有数据集依赖性等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 模型在训练中常出现专家崩溃（dead experts）问题，即部分专家不被路由选择而失效。现有的解决方案依赖手工设计的负载均衡辅助损失函数，缺乏统一的、可预测的调控框架。作者希望引入一个类似于流体力学中雷诺数的无量纲参数，统一诊断和调控MoE训练的专家生态健康度。

Method: 定义控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，其中 T 为路由温度（routing temperature），H 为路由熵权重（routing entropy weight），O 为oracle权重（oracle weight），B 为平衡权重（balance weight）。在8个视觉任务（CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200）和4个语言任务（WikiText-2, WikiText-103）上进行总计超过11,000个训练轮次的对照实验，验证E与死专家比例之间的关系。

Result: E >= 0.5 可保证零死专家，无需额外负载均衡损失。六项额外发现：死专家可被平衡损失驱动复苏；正交毒性具有数据集依赖性而非普遍性；任务复杂性改变E的临界阈值；模型过拟合与专家生态健康无关；三层MoE自发性坍缩为两层功能结构；专家生态结构在50倍温度变化范围内保持不变。

Conclusion: E = T*H/(O+B) 可作为MoE训练的统一诊断参数，类比于流体动力学中的雷诺数，为MoE模型的专家生态健康提供可预测的调控手段。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [375] [INEUS: Iterative Neural Solver for High-Dimensional PIDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06281)
*Jean-Loup Dupret,Davide Gallon,Patrick Cheridito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INEUS是一种无网格迭代神经求解器，通过单跳采样替代非局部跳跃积分显式计算，将偏积分微分方程求解转化为递归回归问题，支持高维线性和非线性PIDEs的高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在处理PIDEs中的非局部跳跃项时计算成本高昂，尤其是在高维问题中。PINNs虽然能学习全局解，但对非局部项的处理效率低且需要计算昂贵的全PDE残差微分。

Method: 采用无网格框架，用单跳采样替代非局部跳跃积分的显式计算；将PIDE求解重新表述为一系列递归回归问题；避免计算完整的PIDE残差微分。

Result: 在多种高维线性和非线性数值实验中，INEUS提供了准确且可扩展的求解结果。对于线性PIDEs，提供基于压缩映射的收敛性证明。

Conclusion: INEUS在处理高维PIDEs方面比PINNs更高效，能够准确求解高维线性和非线性问题，具有理论收敛保证。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce INEUS, a meshfree iterative neural solver for partial integro-differential equations (PIDEs). The method replaces the explicit evaluation of nonlocal jump integrals with single-jump sampling and reformulates PIDE solving as a sequence of recursive regression problems. Like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), INEUS learns global solutions over the entire space-time domain, yet it offers a more efficient treatment of nonlocal terms and avoids the computationally expensive differentiation of full PIDE residuals. These features make INEUS particularly well suited for high-dimensional PDEs and PIDEs. Supported by a contraction-based convergence proof for linear PIDEs, our numerical experiments show that INEUS delivers accurate and scalable solutions for various high-dimensional linear and nonlinear examples.

</details>


### [376] [Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06440)
*Daniel Uyterlinde,Swasti Shreya Mishra,Pascal Mettes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲概念瓶颈模型(HypCBM)，通过将概念激活建模为双曲空间中的非对称几何包含关系，利用蕴含锥的包含边缘产生稀疏且层次感知的激活信号，无需额外监督或学习模块。该方法在稀疏数据下性能媲美需要20倍数据训练的欧氏模型，并具有更强的层次一致性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型(CBM)将概念嵌入平坦欧氏空间，视其为独立正交维度，忽略了概念间固有的层次结构和语义组织关系。

Method: 提出HypCBM框架，将概念激活重新定义为双曲空间中的非对称几何包含关系（蕴含锥）；利用测试时蕴含锥内的包含边缘作为激活信号，无需预训练惩罚项或额外监督；提出自适应缩放法则，实现层次一致的人为干预传播。

Result: 在稀疏数据场景下，HypCBM性能媲美需要20倍数据训练的欧氏后验模型；具有更强的层次一致性和对输入损坏的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双曲空间的几何结构天然适合建模概念层次关系，可替代欧氏空间的独立正交假设，提升CBM的可解释性和数据效率。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have become a popular approach to enable interpretability in neural networks by constraining classifier inputs to a set of human-understandable concepts. While effective, current models embed concepts in flat Euclidean space, treating them as independent, orthogonal dimensions. Concepts, however, are highly structured and organized in semantic hierarchies. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models (HypCBM), a post-hoc framework that grounds the bottleneck in this structure by reformulating concept activation as asymmetric geometric containment in hyperbolic space. Rather than treating entailment cones as a pre-training penalty, we show they encode a natural test-time activation signal: the margin of inclusion within a concept's entailment cone yields sparse, hierarchy-aware activations without any additional supervision or learned modules. We further introduce an adaptive scaling law for hierarchically faithful interventions, propagating user corrections coherently through the concept tree. Empirically, HypCBM rivals post-hoc Euclidean models trained on 20$\times$ more data in sparse regimes required for human interpretability, with stronger hierarchical consistency and improved robustness to input corruptions.

</details>


### [377] [Agentic AIs Are the Missing Paradigm for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06522)
*Xin Wang,Haibo Chen,Wenxuan Liu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了基础模型面临的结构性分布外问题无法在纯模型中心范式下解决，需引入智能体系统作为补充范式。通过形式化定义、参数覆盖天花板定理、智能体系统四属性刻画及反驳七类质疑，提出模型中心与智能体范式协同的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在开放世界部署中面临的知识边界、能力上限、组合偏移及开放任务变化等OOD问题，在结构上不同于传统OOD设定；且预训练与后训练的分布仅部分可观测，现有模型中心方法无法应对。

Method: 1. 提出阶段感知的OOD形式化框架，容纳部分可观测的多阶段训练分布；2. 证明参数覆盖天花板定理，指出存在模型中心方法（无论训练时或测试时）在容差ε内无法处理的输入；3. 刻画智能体OOD系统的四个结构性质：感知、策略选择、外部行动、闭环验证；4. 回应七类反论并指出两项让步。

Result: 证明参数覆盖天花板的存在，表明纯模型中心方法存在固有能力边界；智能体系统通过四个结构性质严格扩展了可处理输入集合；模型中心与智能体范式互补而非替代。

Conclusion: 基础模型的OOD是一个结构独立的问题，需要同时发展模型中心与智能体两种范式。智能体范式应被正式承认并纳入研究议程。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

</details>


### [378] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftSAE 提出了一种动态Top-K稀疏自编码器，通过可微分的Soft Top-K算子让模型根据输入复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量，解决了传统固定稀疏度自编码器无法适应数据复杂度变化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TopK SAE使用固定的稀疏度K，但真实数据在不同样本间的局部固有维度差异很大，固定K在简单输入中引入噪声，在复杂输入中丢失重要结构。需要一种能根据输入复杂度自适应调整特征数量的方法。

Method: 提出SoftSAE，使用可微分的Soft Top-K算子实现动态Top-K选择机制，使模型能够学习与输入相关的稀疏度k，根据每个输入的复杂度自动调整激活特征数量。

Result: SoftSAE不仅能发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确数量的特征。

Conclusion: 动态稀疏度选择能更好地匹配数据的结构，使表示长度反映输入中蕴含的信息量，相比固定稀疏度方法具有优势。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [379] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于约束优化和对偶理论的级联模型决策框架，分析了成本-质量前沿的几何性质，发现级联性能主要受限于结构成本（即级联在升级前需支付廉价模型成本），而非中间阶段不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有级联方法将延迟阈值视为经验超参数，缺乏对成本-质量前沿几何形状的理论指导，需要建立严格的决策理论框架。

Method: 基于约束优化和对偶理论建立决策框架；对两模型级联证明成本-质量前沿的分段凹性；对k模型级联推导一阶条件（单一影子价格均衡各阶段边际质量-成本比）；在5个基准、8个模型上实验验证。

Result: 固定全链不如两两配对级联的逐点包络；优化的子序列级联无实际收益；轻量级预生成路由在4/5数据集上超越最佳级联策略，主要原因是避免了将查询直接发送给大模型时需支付廉价模型的生成成本。

Conclusion: 级联性能的主要限制是结构成本（级联在决定升级前必须支付廉价模型成本），而非中间阶段数量不足。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [380] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了无监督自回归Transformer-VAE在SELFIES分子表示上的潜在空间，发现化学性质的可预测性可能源于序列层面的捷径而非真实化学组织。通过引入残差化、混淆方向对齐分析和解码分子遍历等混淆感知评估方法，证明cLogP等部分化学性质存在鲁棒单调操控方向，但必须通过解码分子验证并控制表征层面的混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成模型常假设潜在空间具有有意义的几何结构，但性质的可预测性可能仅来自序列层面的统计捷径（如SELFIES长度、分支/环标记等），而非真实的化学组织。作者旨在区分真正的化学信号与SELFIES伪影，验证潜在空间中是否存在化学有意义的操控方向。

Method: 使用无监督自回归Transformer-VAE在SELFIES上训练，冻结模型后对RDKit描述符拟合线性探针，将探针权重作为候选全局操控方向。引入混淆感知评估框架：残差化（去除混淆变量影响）、混淆方向对齐分析、解码分子遍历验证。

Result: SELFIES长度、分支标记、环标记和标记熵在潜在空间中被强烈编码。在控制混淆后，cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT和HBA表现出鲁棒的单调操控。非线性探针显示部分性质存在稳定全局方向，其余性质更适合局部潜在梯度描述。

Conclusion: 化学有意义的操控可以在纠缠的分子潜在空间中涌现，但必须通过解码分子验证并控制表征层面的混淆因素，否则可能被序列层面的伪影所干扰。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA. Nonlinear probes further show that some properties admit stable global directions, while others are better described by local latent gradients. Overall, our results show that chemically meaningful steering can emerge in entangled molecular latent spaces, but only when validated through decoded molecules and controlled for representation-level confounds.

</details>


### [381] [Perceive, Route and Modulate: Dynamic Pattern Recalibration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06310)
*Siru Zhong,Zhao Meng,Haohuan Fu,Haoyang Li,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dynamic Pattern Recalibration (DPR)，一种通过"感知-路由-调制"流水线实现token级动态模式重校准的机制，可增强多种时序预测架构，其精简版本DPRNet在12个基准上取得competitive性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度预测模型使用固定权重矩阵对所有时序token统一处理，无法适应实际时间序列中持续变化的局部动态模式，模型只能学到一个折中的平均表示。

Method: 提出DPR机制：通过轻量级"Perceive-Route-Modulate"流水线，计算软路由分布，从学习到的自适应响应模式基中生成时序感知的调制向量，通过残差Hadamard积重校准隐藏状态。

Result: 作为骨干无关的适配器，DPR在多种不同架构上均能提升预测性能且开销极小；作为独立轻量模型DPRNet，在12个基准上取得competitive结果。

Conclusion: 动态模式重校准比宏观参数扩展更有效，证实了token级自适应调制是时序预测的通用瓶颈。

Abstract: Local temporal patterns in real-world time series continuously shift, rendering globally shared transformations suboptimal. Current deep forecasting models, despite their scale and complexity, rely on fixed weight matrices applied uniformly to all temporal tokens. This creates a static pattern response: models settle into a compromised average, unable to adapt to changing local dynamics. We introduce Dynamic Pattern Recalibration (DPR), a backbone-agnostic mechanism that resolves this via token-level recalibration. Through a lightweight "Perceive-Route-Modulate" pipeline, DPR computes a soft-routing distribution over a learned basis of adaptive response patterns, generating a time-aware modulation vector that recalibrates hidden states via a residual Hadamard product. As a backbone-agnostic adapter, DPR enhances forecasting across diverse architectures with minimal overhead, confirming it addresses a general bottleneck. As a minimalist standalone model, DPRNet achieves competitive performance across 12 benchmarks, validating dynamic recalibration against macroscopic parameter scaling.

</details>


### [382] [When Does $\ell_2$-Boosting Overfit Benignly? High-Dimensional Risk Asymptotics and the $\ell_1$ Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06314)
*Ye Su,Jian Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究贪婪集成（ℓ2-Boosting）在ℓ1隐式偏置下的良性过拟合现象。通过耦合凸高斯极小极大定理和双截断高斯矩的渐近展开，解析了非光滑ℓ1插值器。发现在各向同性纯噪声模型下，良性过拟合以对数速率失败（Θ(σ²/log(p/n))），远慢于ℓ2几何下的线性衰减。对于spiked-isotropic设计，风险在对数速率下收敛到零。通过分析非光滑次微分动力学，提出免调参早停规则，在ℓ1-路径有界条件下达到Lasso基本不等式和极小极大最优经验预测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ2几何下的良性过拟合已被充分刻画，但贪婪集成方法（如Boosting）在ℓ1隐式偏置下的行为因其坐标选择阈值的非线性耦合而难以分析，标准谱分解工具失效。本文旨在隔离这种算法偏置，解析高维Boosting的插值风险。

Method: 使用连续时间ℓ2-Boosting框架，结合凸高斯极小极大定理（CGMT）与双截断高斯矩的渐近展开，解析非光滑ℓ1插值器。考虑各向同性纯噪声模型和spiked-isotropic协方差设计，分析风险收敛速率。通过非光滑次微分动力学推导早停规则。

Result: 1) 纯噪声下，良性过拟合以对数速率Θ(σ²/log(p/n))失败，远慢于ℓ2的线性衰减；2) spiked-isotropic设计中，当r₂≫n时风险在对数速率Θ(σ²/log(r₂/n))收敛；3) 提出早停规则，在ℓ1-路径有界条件下恢复Lasso基本不等式并达到极小极大最优速率。

Conclusion: 贪婪Boosting的ℓ1隐式偏置会导致噪声局部化为稀疏活跃集，使过拟合风险仅以对数速率衰减，显著慢于ℓ2几何。早停规则可避开这种慢速收敛，达到最优预测性能。信号-噪声精确分解仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is well-characterized in $\ell_2$ geometries, but its behavior under the $\ell_1$ implicit bias of greedy ensembles remains challenging. The analytical barrier stems from the non-linear coupling of coordinate selection thresholds, which invalidates standard spectral resolvent tools. To isolate this algorithmic bias, we characterize the high-dimensional risk of continuous-time $\ell_2$-Boosting over $p$ features and $n$ samples. By coupling the Convex Gaussian Minimax Theorem with delicate asymptotic expansions of double-sided truncated Gaussian moments, we analytically resolve the non-smooth $\ell_1$ interpolant. Under an isotropic pure-noise model, we prove that benign overfitting fails at the linear rate: greedy selection localizes noise into sparse active sets, and the excess variance decays at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ for noise variance $σ^2$. We remark that while this localization mechanism should persist in the presence of signals, the exact signal-noise decomposition remains an open problem. For spiked-isotropic designs with $k^*$ head eigenvalues and $r_2 = p - k^*$ tail dimensions, the risk converges to zero when $r_{2} \gg n$, but only at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$, which is slower than the linear decay observed in $\ell_2$ geometries. To avoid this slow convergence, we analyze the non-smooth subdifferential dynamics of the boosting flow. This yields a tuning-free early stopping rule that, under a bounded $\ell_1$-path condition, recovers the Lasso basic inequality and attains the minimax-optimal empirical prediction rate for $\ell_1$-bounded signals.

</details>


### [383] [Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06364)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuxPath-FM框架，通过引入服从任意分布的辅助变量来泛化条件流匹配，允许使用高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、离散Rademacher等多种先验分布设计概率路径，并支持标签引导生成等任务，在保持连续性方程和边际训练目标一致性的同时，提供了概率路径设计的通用理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件流匹配方法限制辅助变量为高斯噪声，缺乏灵活性。作者希望设计一个更通用的框架，允许辅助变量服从任意分布，从而实现更多样化的概率路径设计和生成能力。

Method: 提出AuxPath-FM框架，将辅助变量η（服从任意分布）引入概率路径，定义轨迹形式为X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η。理论上证明了该构造保持连续性方程，且训练目标与边际公式一致。实验上探索了高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、离散Rademacher等不同先验分布下的概率路径设计。

Result: 理论证明了AuxPath-FM在任意辅助分布下均能保持连续性方程和边际训练目标一致性；展示了多种先验分布对应的不同几何特性；框架支持标签引导生成等特殊任务。

Conclusion: AuxPath-FM为概率路径设计提供了通用且原理性的基础框架，兼顾理论通用性与实践灵活性，可应用于多种生成式建模任务。

Abstract: We introduce a new generative modeling framework, \textbf{Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths (AuxPath-FM)}, which generalizes conditional flow matching by incorporating an auxiliary variable drawn from an arbitrary distribution into the probability path. Unlike prior methods that restrict auxiliary components to Gaussian noise, AuxPath-FM allows the variable $η$ to follow any distribution, producing trajectories of the form $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$. We theoretically demonstrate that this construction preserves the continuity equation and maintains a training objective consistent with the marginal formulation. This flexibility enables the design of diverse probability paths using various priors, including Gaussian, Uniform, Laplace, and discrete Rademacher distributions, each offering unique geometric properties for generative flows. Furthermore, our framework allows for specialized tasks such as label-guided generation by encoding structured semantic information into the auxiliary distribution. Overall, AuxPath-FM provides a principled and general foundation for probability path design, offering both theoretical generality and practical flexibility for diverse generative modeling tasks.

</details>


### [384] [Eliciting associations between clinical variables from LLMs via comparison questions across populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06335)
*Fabian Kabus,Kian Kordtomeikel,Thomas Brox,Heinz Wiendl,Daiana Stolz,Harald Binder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化比较问题（患者比较三元组问题）的间接提取方法，结合统计模型和不变因果预测（ICP），从大语言模型中恢复患者特征之间的相关性和因果联系，并在COPD和MS两个临床领域验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练数据包含大量生物医学文献，反映了不同患者群体的数据，但直接 eliciting 信息存在陷阱。作者希望探索如何从LLM中间接恢复患者特征之间的相关性和因果链接，以支持医疗决策。

Method: 提出基于患者比较三元组问题的结构化比较方法，结合统计模型估计相关性（无需访问激活值或模型内部）。通过提示级环境偏移获取不同子群体的相关性估计，再应用不变因果预测（ICP）获得保守的候选父节点因果链接。

Result: 在COPD和MS两个临床领域，不同提示环境下提取的相关性平滑、稳定且具有临床可解释性，同时存在统计显著的变异，支持下游不变性检验；ICP提供了少量候选不变父节点链接。

Conclusion: 通过三元组比较的间接提取方法能够从LLM中恢复有意义的关联结构，提供了一条从隐式相关性到与LLM回答模式一致的因果陈述的谨慎路径。

Abstract: The training data of large language models (LLMs) comprises a wide range of biomedical literature, reflecting data from many different patient populations. We investigate how it might be possible to recover information on correlation and causal links between patient characteristics, as a key building block for medical decision making. To avoid the pitfalls of direct elicitation, we propose an approach based on structured comparison questions, specifically patient comparison triplet questions. This is combined with a statistical model for the LLM representation that provides estimates of correlations without access to activations or model internals. Intuitively, we consider how similarity decisions of LLMs based on a first variable are affected by providing information on a second variable for one of the patients being assessed. We then induce prompt-level environment shifts to obtain correlation estimates for different subpopulations, which enables an invariant causal prediction (ICP) approach to obtain conservative candidate parent links. We demonstrate the method in two clinical domains, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). Across prompted environments, the elicited correlations are smooth, stable, and clinically interpretable, yet vary in a statistically significant way that supports downstream invariance testing, such that ICP provides a small set of candidate invariant parent links. These results show that indirect elicitation via triplet comparisons can recover meaningful association structure from LLMs and offer a cautious route from implicit correlations to causal statements that are congruent with LLM answering patterns.

</details>


### [385] [Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation: Bridging Exploitation and Imitation at the Token Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06387)
*Nan Jia,Haojin Yang,Xing Ma,Jiesong Lian,Shuailiang Zhang,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Zequn Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称同策略蒸馏（AOPD），通过在非正优势区域采用局部散度最小化替代无效负强化，克服了标准同策略蒸馏（OPD）在优势加权策略梯度中的高方差、零优势区域梯度消失及探索瓶颈问题，在数学推理任务上相比OPD平均提升4.09%（强初始化）和8.34%（弱初始化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准同策略蒸馏（OPD）虽优于异策略蒸馏和标准强化学习，但其优势加权策略梯度存在三个结构性缺陷：高方差更新、零优势区域梯度消失以及纠错信号不足时的探索瓶颈，限制了其性能。

Method: 提出非对称同策略蒸馏（AOPD），在非正优势区域采用局部散度最小化替代无效的负强化，同时保留正优势区域的强化学习机制，形成非对称的训练策略。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，AOPD在强/弱初始化下分别平均提升4.09%/8.34%，同时保持更高的策略熵和更好的工具适应能力保留。

Conclusion: AOPD通过非对称设计有效解决了OPD的结构性缺陷，在数学推理任务上取得一致性提升，并展现出更好的探索性和能力保持特性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories with token-level teacher feedback and often outperforms off-policy distillation and standard reinforcement learning. However, we find that its standard advantage weighted policy gradient suffers from three structural weaknesses, including high variance updates, vanishing gradients in zero-advantage regions, and exploration bottlenecks when corrective signals are insufficient.We therefore propose Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which replaces ineffective negative reinforcement with localized divergence minimization in non-positive advantage regions while preserving positive reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that AOPD consistently outperforms standard OPD, with average gains of 4.09 / 8.34 under strong / weak initialization, respectively. AOPD also maintains higher policy entropy during training and better capability retention during sequential tool-use adaptation.

</details>


### [386] [Consistent Geometric Deep Learning via Hilbert Bundles and Cellular Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06395)
*Kartik Tandon,Julian Gould,Tanishq Bhatia,Francesca Dominici,Alejandro Ribeiro,Claudio Battiloro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HilbNets，一种基于希尔伯特丛上连接拉普拉斯算子的卷积学习框架，适用于流形上无限维信号（如时序列、概率分布、算子）。通过两步采样策略实现可计算性：首先证明流形采样收敛到希尔伯特细胞层（推广了Belkin & Niyogi的图拉普拉斯收敛结果），然后证明离散化HilbNets收敛到连续架构且可迁移。实验验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构越来越多地处理本质上是无限维的复杂信号（如时序列、概率分布、算子），这些信号定义在不规则域上，但缺乏统一的学习理论。本文旨在填补这一空白，将经典拉普拉斯框架推广到每个点的信号位于其自身希尔伯特空间的场景。

Method: 提出HilbNets框架：1) 使用与希尔伯特丛关联的连接拉普拉斯作为卷积算子；2) 通过两步采样实现：首先流形采样诱导希尔伯特细胞层，证明其层拉普拉斯概率收敛到连接拉普拉斯（推广了Belkin & Niyogi的经典结果）；然后离散化信号并证明离散HilbNets收敛到连续架构且跨采样可迁移。

Result: 1) 证明了从流形采样得到的希尔伯特细胞层的层拉普拉斯在概率上收敛到连接拉普拉斯；2) 证明了离散化HilbNets收敛到底层连续架构，且在不同采样间具有可迁移性；3) 在合成和实际任务上验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过将经典拉普拉斯框架推广到信号位于其自身希尔伯特空间的场景，拓宽了几何学习的范围，为无限维信号处理提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Modern deep learning architectures increasingly contend with sophisticated signals that are natively infinite-dimensional, such as time series, probability distributions, or operators, and are defined over irregular domains. Yet, a unified learning theory for these settings has been lacking. To start addressing this gap, we introduce a novel convolutional learning framework for possibly infinite-dimensional signals supported on a manifold. Namely, we use the connection Laplacian associated with a Hilbert bundle as a convolutional operator, and we derive filters and neural networks, dubbed as \textit{HilbNets}. We make HilbNets and, more generally, the convolution operation, implementable via a two-stage sampling procedure. First, we show that sampling the manifold induces a Hilbert Cellular Sheaf, a generalized graph structure with Hilbert feature spaces and edge-wise coupling rules, and we prove that its sheaf Laplacian converges in probability to the underlying connection Laplacian as the sampling density increases. Notably, this result is a generalization to the infinite-dimensional bundle setting of the Belkin \& Niyogi \cite{BELKIN20081289} convergence result for the graph Laplacian to the manifold Laplacian, a theoretical cornerstone of geometric learning methods. Second, we discretize the signals and prove that the discretized (implementable) HilbNets converge to the underlying continuous architectures and are transferable across different samplings of the same bundle, providing consistency for learning. Finally, we validate our framework on synthetic and real-world tasks. Overall, our results broaden the scope of geometric learning as a whole by lifting classical Laplacian-based frameworks to settings where the signal at each point lives in its own Hilbert space.

</details>


### [387] [Preliminary Insights in Chronos Frequency Data Understanding and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06361)
*Alessandro Pagani,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Sergio Benini,Francesco Gringoli,Mattia Savardi,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初步分析了Chronos基础模型处理及内部表示频域信息的能力。通过在受控条件下使用离散正弦信号和最小描述长度探针，测试了模型内部表示中频率信息的存在性和可分离性，揭示了频率信息在不同频段的捕获特性及表示质量退化的区域，为信号处理和信息融合场景中Chronos的使用提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管时间序列基础模型被广泛采用，但这类模型编码基本信号特性的程度尚未得到充分刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统分析Chronos模型对频域信息的内部表征能力。

Method: 使用受控条件下生成的离散正弦信号（固定频率），并采用轻量级在线最小描述长度探针（Minimum Description Length probes）作用于解码器架构，测试模型内部表示中频率信息的存在性和可分离性。

Result: 揭示了频域内容如何在不同频段被捕获，并指出了表示质量可能退化或需要特别关注的区域。

Conclusion: 为Chronos用户在信号处理和信息融合场景中提供了实践指导，并有助于推进时间序列基础模型可解释性与评估的持续改进工作。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary analysis of the ability of Chronos foundation model to process and internally represent frequency domain information. Foundation models that process time-series data offer practitioners a unified architecture capable of learning generic temporal representations across diverse tasks and domains, reducing the need for task-specific feature engineering and enabling transfer across signal modalities. Despite their growing adoption, the extent to which such models encode fundamental signal properties remains insufficiently characterised. We address this gap by analysing Chronos under controlled conditions, starting from the simplest class of signals: discrete sinusoids generated at fixed frequencies. Using lightweight online minimum description length probes applied to the decoder architecture, we test for the presence and separability of frequency information in the model's internal representations. The results provide insight into how frequential content is captured across the frequency spectrum and highlight regimes in which representation quality may degrade or require particular care. These findings offer practical guidance for users of Chronos in signal processing and information fusion contexts, and contribute to ongoing efforts to improve the interpretability and evaluation of foundation models for temporal data.

</details>


### [388] [Operator-Guided Invariance Learning for Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06500)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VPSD-RL框架，通过Lie群作用与拉回算子发现连续强化学习中的值保持结构，包括精确和近似结构，并证明其稳定性保证，在连续控制任务中提升数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间/状态/动作空间的强化学习常面临数据效率低、对干扰和漂移敏感的问题，现有方法多局限于预设对称性和精确等变性，缺乏发现更一般值保持结构的通用方法。

Method: 将连续RL建模为受控扩散过程，通过Lie群作用和拉回算子定义值保持映射；证明值保持结构存在当且仅当拉回值函数与前推动作与受控生成元和奖励泛函可交换；基于Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman失配量小可找到近似值保持结构；通过拟合可微漂移/扩散/奖励模型、确定方程残差最小化学无穷小生成元、ODE流指数映射得有限变换、结合过渡增强和变换一致性正则化集成到连续RL中。

Result: 有界生成元/奖励失配意味着最优值函数沿近似轨道具有定量稳定性，灵敏度由有效视界控制；在连续控制基准上观察到数据效率和鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: VPSD-RL提供了一种系统发现连续RL中精确和近似值保持结构的通用框架，兼具理论保证和实践有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with continuous time and state/action spaces is often data-intensive and brittle under nuisance variability and shift, motivating methods that exploit value-preserving structures to stabilize and improve learning. Most existing approaches focus on special cases, such as prescribed symmetries and exact equivariance, without addressing how to discover more general structures that require nonlinear operators to transform and map between continuous state/action systems with isomorphic value functions. We propose \textbf{VPSD-RL} (Value-Preserving Structure Discovery for Reinforcement Learning). It models continuous RL as a controlled diffusion with value-preserving mappings defined through Lie-group actions and associated pullback operators. We show that a value-preserving structure exists exactly when pulling back the value function and pushing forward actions commute with the controlled generator and reward functional. Further, approximate value-preserving structures with rigorous guarantees can be found when the Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman mismatch is small. This framework discovers exact and approximate value-preserving structures by searching for the associated Lie group operators. VPSD-RL fits differentiable drift, diffusion, and reward models; learns infinitesimal generators via determining-equation residual minimization; exponentiates them with ODE flows to obtain finite transformations; and integrates them into continuous RL through transition augmentation and transformation-consistency regularization. We show that bounded generator/reward mismatch implies quantitative stability of the optimal value function along approximate orbits, with sensitivity governed by the effective horizon, and observe improved data efficiency and robustness on continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [389] [Data-Driven Covariate Selection for Nonparametric and Cycle-Agnostic Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06385)
*Ana Leticia Garcez Vicente,Gijs van Seeventer,Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于条件独立性的局部数据驱动协变量选择方法，证明其在循环因果模型中同样保持完备性，建立了一个统一的无环/有环因果效应估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果效应估计方法通常假设无环性（acyclicity）或依赖全局因果结构学习，在处理存在潜在混淆和反馈循环的现实场景时计算效率低且适用性有限。因此，需要一种局部、数据驱动且适用于循环因果模型的协变量选择方法。

Method: 基于条件独立性信息的局部数据驱动协变量选择方法，利用σ-无环化（σ-acyclification）下条件独立断言的不变性，将无环因果模型中的完备性证明推广到循环因果模型。

Result: 理论上证明该方法在循环因果模型中同样保持有效性和完备性；在大量合成数据上进行的实证验证表明其在循环因果模型中表现可靠。

Conclusion: 建立了一个统一的、与因果图中是否有环无关的协变量选择和因果效应估计视角，证明该局部方法可无需修改直接适用于循环和无环两种设置。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data requires identifying valid adjustment sets. This task is especially challenging in realistic settings where latent confounding and feedback loops are present. Existing approaches typically assume acyclicity or rely on global causal structure learning, limiting applicability and computational efficiency. In this work, we study a local, data-driven method for covariate selection based on conditional independence information. While this method is known to be sound and complete in acyclic causal models, its validity in the presence of cycles has remained unclear. Our main contribution is to show that these guarantees extend to cyclic causal models. In particular, our result relies on the invariance of conditional independence assertions under $σ$-acyclification. These findings establish a unified, cycle-agnostic perspective on covariate selection and causal effect estimation, showing that the method applies across cyclic and acyclic settings without modification. Empirically, we validate this on extensive synthetic data, showing reliable performance in cyclic causal models.

</details>


### [390] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR的推理能力主要集中在秩1分量上，并观察到隐式奖励过拟合现象，同时揭示了RLVR的三个关键特性：仅保留数学推理能力、优化特定奇异谱（服从重尾分布）、以及左奇异向量在训练中呈现强对齐趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）如何塑造模型参数，揭示其内部机制，为改进现有强化学习范式或实现持续学习提供潜在见解。

Method: 采用周期性秩1替换方法，通过分析秩1分量、奇异值分布和左奇异向量对齐趋势，系统研究RLVR对模型参数的影响。

Result: (1) RLVR中的有效秩1分量除数学推理能力外不保留其他模型知识；(2) RLVR通过优化特定奇异谱发挥作用，几乎所有线性层的奇异值分布呈重尾分布；(3) 秩1分量相关的左奇异向量在训练中呈现更强的对齐趋势，表明RLVR本质上优化采样效率。

Conclusion: RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合现象，其推理能力集中体现在秩1分量中，这些发现揭示了RLVR塑造模型参数的机制，为改进现有RL范式及实现持续学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [391] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度学习优化中存在方向对齐与损失收敛可解耦的现象，并提出GONO优化器，通过基于梯度方向一致性（cc_t）自适应调整Adam的动量系数beta_1，在保持收敛率的同时实现更好的优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的优化器（如Adam、SGD、RMSprop）缺乏利用梯度方向时间一致性的显式机制，依赖基于幅度的信号，无法有效区分平台区、鞍点和真正收敛，导致方向对齐与损失收敛可能解耦。

Method: 提出GONO（Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer），根据连续梯度余弦相似度cc_t自适应调整Adam的动量系数beta_1：在方向一致时放大动量，在振荡时抑制动量，并证明了其与Adam相同的O(1/sqrt(T))收敛率。

Result: cc_t在振荡检测中F1=1.00（梯度范数仅0.45）；GONO在MNIST（98.15%）、CIFAR-10（43.14%）和ResNet-18（75.44%）上与AdamW保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 方向对齐是一个有理论依据且实用的优化信号，GONO证明了利用该信号的有效性。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [392] [SparseForge: Efficient Semi-Structured LLM Sparsification via Annealing of Hessian-Guided Soft-Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06402)
*Liu Hanzuo,Chaofan Lin,Weixuan Sun,Yulong Wang,Key,Rayying,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseForge是一种面向半结构化稀疏性的后训练框架，通过直接优化稀疏掩码而非扩大重训练数据量，显著提升LLM的稀疏恢复效率。在LLaMA-2-7B的2:4稀疏性下，仅用5B tokens即超越稠密模型精度，逼近使用40B tokens的SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化稀疏性有硬件加速优势，但后训练剪枝因结构耦合导致精度下降严重；现有方法依赖大规模稀疏重训练来恢复精度，计算成本高昂。

Method: 将Hessian感知的重要性估计与软掩码渐进退火相结合，直接优化稀疏掩码以生成硬件可执行的结构化稀疏性。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B的2:4稀疏性下，使用5B重训练tokens达到57.27%零样本准确率，超过稠密模型的56.43%，接近SOTA方法使用40B tokens的57.52%。

Conclusion: SparseForge在精度-效率权衡上取得了一致性改进，且跨模型系列具有泛化能力。

Abstract: Semi-structured sparsity provides a practical path to accelerate large language models (LLMs) with native hardware support, but post-training semi-structured pruning often suffers from substantial quality degradation due to strong structural coupling. Existing methods rely on large-scale sparse retraining to recover accuracy, resulting in high computational cost.
  We propose SparseForge, a post-training framework that improves recovery efficiency by directly optimizing the sparsity mask rather than scaling up retraining tokens. SparseForge combines Hessian-aware importance estimation with progressive annealing of soft masks into hardware-executable structured sparsity, enabling stable and efficient sparse recovery. On LLaMA-2-7B under 2:4 sparsity, SparseForge achieves 57.27% average zero-shot accuracy with only $\textbf{5B}$ retraining tokens, surpassing the dense model's 56.43% accuracy and approaching the 57.52% result of a state-of-the-art method using $\textbf{40B}$ tokens. Such improvements on the accuracy-efficiency trade-off from SparseForge are shown to be consistent across model families.

</details>


### [393] [Towards Metric-Faithful Neural Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06588)
*Jyotirmaya Shivottam,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了编码器几何结构对神经图编辑距离（GED）估计的影响，证明双Lipschitz编码器能提升GED预测与排序性能，并基于此设计了FSW-GNN作为即插即用模块，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经图匹配架构在GED估计上取得了显著进展，但编码器几何结构在其中的作用尚不明确，缺乏系统的理论理解。

Method: 建立理论框架，将编码器几何与两类GED估计器（图相似性预测器和基于对齐的方法）的质量联系起来；提出图级和节点级双Lipschitz几何条件；设计FSW-GNN作为双Lipschitz且与WL等价的编码器，作为即插即用替换模块。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，基于FSW-GNN的几何感知变体显著提升了GED预测和排序指标；消融实验和迁移实验证实性能提升源于改进的表示几何结构。

Conclusion: 编码器几何结构是神经图匹配中有用的设计原则，双Lipschitz编码器能有效改善GED估计质量。

Abstract: Graph Edit Distance (GED) is a fundamental, albeit NP-hard, metric for structural graph similarity. Recent neural graph matching architectures approximate GED by first encoding graphs with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and then applying either a graph-level regression head or a matching-based alignment module. Despite substantial architectural progress, the role of encoder geometry in neural GED estimation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that connects encoder geometry to GED estimation quality for two broad classes of neural GED estimators: graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods. On fixed graph collections, where the doubly-stochastic metric $d_{\mathrm{DS}}$ is comparable to GED, we show that graph-level bi-Lipschitz encoders yield controlled GED surrogates and improved ranking stability; for matching-based estimators, node-level bi-Lipschitz geometry propagates to encoder-induced alignment costs and the resulting optimized alignment objective. We instantiate this perspective using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz WL-equivalent encoder, as a drop-in replacement in representative neural GED architectures. Across representative baselines and benchmark datasets, the resulting geometry-aware variants significantly improve GED prediction and ranking metrics. A faithfulness case study of untrained encoders, together with ablations and transfer experiments, supports the view that these gains arise from improved representation geometry, positioning encoder geometry as a useful design principle for neural graph matching.

</details>


### [394] [FRInGe: Distribution-Space Integrated Gradients with Fisher--Rao Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06404)
*Gabriele Martino,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fisher-Rao积分梯度(FRInGe)方法，通过在预测分布空间而非输入空间定义参考点和插值路径，解决原始积分梯度方法对基线选择、直线路径、离散化和饱和的脆弱性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 积分梯度(IG)方法虽具有模型忠实性和可扩展性，但其解释结果因启发式基线、直线路径、离散化和饱和而存在脆弱性，亟需改进。

Method: FRInGe在预测分布空间中定义最大熵预测参考点，并沿概率单纯形上的Fisher-Rao测地线构建插值轨迹；通过拉回Fisher度量实现对应的输入空间轨迹，并利用KL散度和欧几里得信任域稳定该过程；最后沿此轨迹对输入梯度积分得到归因结果。

Result: 在六个ImageNet架构上，FRInGe在面向校准的归因指标（尤其是MAS分数）上提升最为显著，同时在扰动AUC和不忠实度指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: FRInGe通过在预测分布空间操作有效缓解了IG的固有问题，在归因校准性方面取得显著改进，同时不牺牲其他关键性能指标。

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution methods are model-faithful and scalable, but Integrated Gradients (IG) can be brittle because explanations depend on heuristic baselines, straight-line paths, discretization, and saturation. We propose Fisher--Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe), which defines both the reference and interpolation schedule in predictive distribution space. FRInGe replaces input baselines with a maximum-entropy predictive reference and follows a Fisher-Rao geodesic on the probability simplex. The corresponding input-space trajectory is realized through the pullback Fisher metric and stabilized by KL and Euclidean trust regions; attributions are obtained by integrating input gradients along this trajectory. Across six ImageNet architectures, FRInGe most clearly improves calibration-oriented attribution metrics, especially MAS scores, while remaining competitive on perturbation AUC and infidelity.

</details>


### [395] [Federated Cross-Client Subgraph Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06433)
*Selin Ceydeli,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分布式图场景下GNN子图模式检测中存在的表示等价鸿沟问题，本文将其形式化为结构可观测性问题，并提出每步逐层嵌入交换框架，通过跨客户端同步中间节点表示来恢复与集中式GNN相同的表示能力。实验证明嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合互补而非可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的子图模式检测方法假设全局图集中可用，但在图数据分布在多个参与方的情况下，客户端本地GNN计算会偏离集中式模型，产生表示等价鸿沟（representation-equivalence gap），即跨分区的子图模式在本地不可识别。

Method: 提出每步逐层嵌入交换框架（per-step, layer-wise embedding exchange framework）：在前向传播的每一层，客户端同步交换中间节点表示（embeddings），不暴露原始特征或标签；基于扩展子图假设（extended-subgraph assumption）和跨客户端共享模型参数，该框架能恢复与全图集中式GNN相同的节点表示。

Result: 在包含循环、双团和散聚模式的合成有向多重图上实验表明：嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合是互补而非可互换的机制；两者结合能恢复大部分表示鸿沟，前提是交换的嵌入是每步新鲜（per-step）而非每轮过时（per-epoch）。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了分布式GNN中表示等价鸿沟的结构可观测性本质，证明了逐层嵌入交换机制的有效性，并指出联邦学习中的参数聚合与嵌入交换各自解决不同层面的分布式学习挑战，需要协同使用。

Abstract: Subgraph pattern detection aims to uncover complex interaction structures in graphs. However, state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN)-based solutions assume centralized access to the entire graph. When graphs are instead distributed across multiple parties, client-local GNN computations diverge from those of a centralized model, resulting in a representation-equivalence gap. We formalize this as a structural observability problem, where subgraph patterns crossing partition boundaries become locally unidentifiable. To bridge this gap, we propose a per-step, layer-wise embedding exchange framework in which clients synchronize intermediate node representations at each layer of the forward pass, without exposing raw features or labels. Under an extended-subgraph assumption and shared model parameters across clients, this framework recovers the same node representations as a centralized GNN over the full graph. Experiments on synthetic directed multigraphs with cycles, bicliques, and scatter-gather patterns show that embedding exchange and federated parameter aggregation are complementary rather than interchangeable: their combination recovers most of the representation gap, provided exchanged embeddings are fresh per-step rather than stale per-epoch.

</details>


### [396] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了在ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞光滑性和可分离噪声假设下，SignSGD相比SGD在稀疏噪声条件下可实现维度因子d的加速，并将该框架推广到矩阵域证明Muon优化器具有相同的最优维度缩放，最后在124M参数GPT-2预训练中验证了理论优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于符号的优化算法（如SignSGD、Muon）在训练大规模基础模型中表现优异，但缺乏理论理解——在标准光滑性和有限方差条件下SGD已被证明是极小极大最优的，这从根本上排除了符号方法在标准设置中的复杂度优势。因此作者希望突破这一障碍，建立符号优化器优于SGD的理论框架。

Method: 提出使用ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞光滑性和可分离噪声模型来刻画符号更新的坐标级性质。在该问题几何下，推导SignSGD的匹配上下界，通过与SGD下界比较量化复杂度优势。将该框架扩展到矩阵域，证明Muon优化器保持了最优维度缩放。最后通过124M参数GPT-2预训练实验验证理论预测。

Result: 理论结果表明，SignSGD在稀疏噪声条件下可将复杂度降低d倍（d为问题维度）。Muon优化器在矩阵域推广中保持相同的最优维度缩放。GPT-2预训练实验证实了SignSGD的理论优势准确预测了其更快收敛。

Conclusion: 本文建立了符号优化算法超越SGD的严格理论基础，揭示了ℓ₁范数平稳性等新几何视角的重要性，并成功将理论框架从向量域推广至矩阵域，为理解实际大规模训练中符号方法的高效性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [397] [FedFrozen: Two-Stage Federated Optimization via Attention Kernel Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06446)
*Junye Du,Zhenghao Li,Yushi Feng,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedFrozen两阶段联邦优化框架：先全模型预热训练，然后冻结注意力机制的查询/键模块仅优化值模块，以缓解异构联邦学习中的客户端漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦优化方法通过目标正则化或更新校正等机制应对客户端异构性带来的漂移问题，但近期发现Transformer架构在异构联邦训练中具有内在鲁棒性。作者希望探究注意力机制中不同参数组件对联邦优化的具体影响，并据此设计更优的优化策略。

Method: 将注意力模块分解为查询/键块（决定注意力核）和值块（在诱导核下执行语义变换），提出两阶段框架FedFrozen：第一阶段进行全模型预热训练，第二阶段冻结查询/键块仅优化值块。在线性注意力形式下，证明预热阶段可视为正则化核分布目标上的非精确下降过程，冻结阶段简化为固定注意力核下的受约束值块优化问题。

Result: 仿真实验验证了预期的偏差-漂移行为；真实数据实验表明FedFrozen提升了Transformer模型在异构联邦学习中的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: 注意力机制中查询/键块与值块扮演不同角色，冻结查询/键块能有效缓解客户端漂移，且预热长度存在显式权衡。

Abstract: Federated learning with heterogeneous clients remains a significant challenge for deep learning, primarily due to client drift arising from inconsistent local updates. Existing federated optimization methods typically address this issue through objective-level regularization or update-correction mechanisms. Recent studies, however, suggest that Transformer-based architectures may be inherently more robust than conventional models under heterogeneous federated training. Motivated by this observation, we investigate how different parameter components within the attention mechanism influence federated optimization. Specifically, we decompose the attention module into a query/key block, which determines the attention kernel, and a value block, which performs semantic transformation under the induced kernel. Based on this perspective, we propose FedFrozen, a two-stage federated optimization framework that first performs full-model warm-up training and then freezes the query/key block while continuing to optimize the value block. Under a linear-attention formulation, we show that the warm-up stage can be interpreted as an inexact descent procedure on a regularized kernel-profile objective, while the frozen stage reduces to a restricted value-block optimization problem under a fixed attention kernel. Our analysis further reveals an explicit trade-off that governs the choice of warm-up length. Simulations validate the predicted bias-drift behavior, and real-data experiments demonstrate that FedFrozen improves both the stability and effectiveness of Transformer models in heterogeneous federated learning.

</details>


### [398] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归智能体优化（RAO），一种训练能递归生成子智能体并委派子任务的递归智能体的强化学习方法，实现推理时缩放，提升长上下文处理能力和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单智能体系统在处理超长上下文和复杂任务时存在局限性，需要一种能通过分治策略自动扩展推理能力的方法。

Method: 采用强化学习框架RAO，训练递归智能体学习何时以及如何委派子任务并与其他子智能体通信，实现递归式分治推理。

Result: 递归智能体在训练效率、超越上下文窗口的任务扩展、向更难任务的泛化以及时钟时间减少方面均优于单智能体系统。

Conclusion: RAO可有效训练递归智能体，使其通过递归委派实现推理时缩放，显著提升对复杂、长上下文任务的适应能力。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [399] [Scene-Adaptive Continual Learning for CSI-based Human Activity Recognition with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06447)
*Wenhan Zheng,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAMoE-C，一种基于场景自适应混合专家模型（含聚类专家）的跨域CSI人体活动识别方法，通过注意力路由选择性激活专家，配合轻量训练协议，在保持高精度的同时显著降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CSI的HAR在不同物理环境（域漂移）下性能下降严重，持续学习方法随域积累扩展性差、依赖大重放缓冲区或推理成本线性增长，亟需一种高效可扩展的跨域解决方案。
